filtering is presentation-only (no access-control enforcement on the stream itself — that's handled by LogStore's existing RLS policy)\n\n### Accessibility Compliance Summary\n\n- ✓ Native button elements (role=button) for all interactive nodes\n- ✓ aria-pressed for toggle state\n- ✓ aria-expanded for disclosure (future: domain grouping)\n- ✓ aria-label for all nodes (name + route + status + counts)\n- ✓ aria-live for error announcements\n- ✓ Visible :focus-visible rings on all focusable elements\n- ✓ prefers-reduced-motion disables animations\n- ✓ WCAG AA contrast for all text + status colors\n- ✓ Keyboard: Tab, Enter, Space, Arrow, Home/End\n- ✓ Playwright tests use getByRole/getByLabel\n\n## Questions & Follow-ups\n\nNone at this time — the design spec and dummy are comprehensive and approved by Phil. Implementation guidance is clear; constraints are explicit. Every\n16\t  `Nebu.Admin.TraceMapGeometry.<fun>(...)` call below fails with\n17\t  `** (UndefinedFunctionError)`, NOT a compile error in this file (Elixir\n18\t  does not check a remote function exists at compile time).\n19\t\n20\t  ## Module contract this file pins down (proposed — the dev agent may\n21\t  restructure the public API, e.g. return structs instead of plain maps, or\n22\t  fold these five functions into fewer/more, AS LONG AS the SVG rendered by\n23\t  `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`'s `render/1` places every node at exactly\n24\t  the (x, y) this contract computes — see\n25\t  `trace_map_leitstand_test.exs`'s \"AC2: radial SVG map\" describe block for\n26\t  the rendered-output cross-check that keeps this file honest against the\n27\t  real template):\n28\t\n29\t  Constants (spec §2, viewBox `-560 -400 1120 800`):\n30\t\n31\t    * `@center_radius` 52 — nebu's own circle radius, always at `{0.0, 0.0}`.\n32\t    * `@domain_radius` 38 — every domain circle's radius.\n33\t    * `@satellite_radius` 24 — every satellite circle's radius.\n34\t    * `@domain_ring` 195.0 — R1, the ring domains sit on (measured from nebu).\n35\t    * `@satellite_ring` 128.0 — R2, the ring satellites sit on (measured from\n36\t      THEIR OWN domain's center, per the dummy's `edge()`/satellite-placement\n37\t      code — NOT from nebu).\n38\t    * `@edge_padding` 4 — trimmed off each end of an edge line, on top of the\n39\t      node's own radius.\n40\t\n41\t  Functions:\n42\t\n43\t    * `domain_angles(domain_keys :: [String.t()]) :: [{String.t(), float()}]`\n44\t      — one `{domain_key, angle_degrees}` pair per input key, SAME order as\n45\t      input (first-observed order, never re-sorted).\n46\t\n47\t      Canonical rule (spec §2's literal 4 named domains): when\n48\t      `domain_keys == [\"/_matrix/client\", \"/_matrix/media\", \"/admin\",\n49\t      \"/health\"]` EXACTLY (this order), the angles are the spec's own fixed\n50\t      values `[-48, 48, 132, -132]` — NOT evenly spaced (132 - (-132) = 264,\n51\t      not 90; this is a deliberately hand-placed, not a uniform, layout).\n52\t\n53\t      Fallback rule (spec §2: \"distribute evenly if domain count differs\" —\n54\t      also covers the canonical 4 domains appearing in ANY OTHER order, or\n55\t      with a 5th domain, etc.): evenly distributed starting at `-90.0`\n56\t      degrees, stepping by `360.0 / length(domain_keys)` per domain, i.e.\n57\t      `angle(i) = -90.0 + i * (360.0 / n)` for `i` in `0..(n - 1)`.\n58\t\n59\t    * `satellite_angles(domain_angle :: float(), count :: pos_integer()) ::\n60\t      [float()]` — `count` angles fanned around `domain_angle`, spread\n61\t      `min(130, count * 26)` degrees (spec §2's `min(130°, n·26°)`):\n62\t\n63\t        * `count == 1` → `[domain_angle]` (centered exactly on the domain,\n64\t          no spread — dividing by `count - 1` would be a division by zero).\n65\t        * `count > 1` → `domain_angle - spread / 2 + i * (spread /\n66\t          (count - 1))` for `i` in `0..(count - 1)` (evenly fanned across\n67\t          the full spread, first satellite at `domain_angle - spread / 2`,\n68\t          last at `domain_angle + spread / 2`).\n69\t\n70\t    * `polar_to_cartesian(angle_degrees :: float(), radius :: number()) ::\n71\t      {float(), float()}` — `{radius * :math.cos(radians), radius *\n72\t      :math.sin(radians)}`, standard SVG convention (positive Y is down,\n73\t      but this function does no Y-flip — same as the dummy's own `Math.cos`/\n74\t      `Math.sin` usage).\n75\t\n76\t    * `trim_edge(a :: %{x: number(), y: number(), r: number()}, b :: %{x:\n77\t      number(), y: number(), r: number()}) :: {float(), float(), float(),\n78\t      float()}` — `{x1, y1, x2, y2}` for a straight line from `a` to `b`,\n79\t      shortened at EACH end by that end's own `r + @edge_padding` (spec §2\n80\t      \"node edge + 4px\"; mirrors the dummy's `edge()` function exactly).\n81\t\n82\t    * `layout(domains :: [%{key: String.t(), satellites: [String.t()]}]) ::\n83\t      %{nebu: map(), domains: %{String.t() => map()}, satellites:\n84\t      %{String.t() => map()}}` — the full computed tree: `nebu` is always\n85\t      `%{x: 0.0, y: 0.0, r: 52}`; each domain map has `:x`/`:y`/`:r`/\n86\t      `:angle`; each satellite map has `:x`/`:y`/`:r`/`:angle`/`:domain`\n87\t      (its parent's key) — satellite absolute position is the PARENT\n88\t      DOMAIN's position plus the satellite's own polar offset (see\n89\t      `satellite_ring`'s doc above), not an offset from the origin.\n90\t  \"\"\"\n91\t\n92\t  use ExUnit.Case, async: true\n93\t\n94\t  alias Nebu.Admin.TraceMapGeometry, as: Geometry\n95\t\n96\t  @canonical_domains [\"/_matrix/client\", \"/_matrix/media\", \"/admin\", \"/health\"]\n97\t\n98\t  # `apply/3` (not a direct `Geometry.foo(...)` remote call) is used for\n99\t  # EVERY call into the not-yet-existing `Nebu.Admin.TraceMapGeometry`\n100\t  # below. `mix test --warnings-as-errors` (make test-unit-elixir) runs\n101\t  # `mix xref`-backed \"undefined remote function\" checks on ordinary direct\n102\t  # calls to a module known not to define that function anywhere in this\n103\t  # project — which would turn this file's entire RED-phase INTENT (fail at\n104\t  # runtime, not fail to compile) into a hard compile error instead. This is\n105\t  # a genuinely different situation from this repo's existing\n106\t  # `Phoenix.Component`/LiveView ATDD precedents (`trace_map_bubble_test.exs`'s\n107\t  # `render_component(&Mod.fun/1, ...)` capture, `logs_live_test.exs`'s\n108\t  # `live_isolated(conn, Mod, ...)` module-atom-as-data) — `Geometry` is a\n109\t  # PLAIN module with no such indirection available, so `apply/3` (the\n110\t  # standard, fully-dynamic escape hatch `mix xref` cannot see through) is\n111\t  # the correct RED-phase-safe equivalent for a plain-function module\n112\t  # contract like this one.\n113\t  defp domain_angles(keys), do: apply(Geometry, :domain_angles, [keys])\n114\t  defp satellite_angles(angle, count), do: apply(Geometry, :satellite_angles, [angle, count])\n115\t  defp polar_to_cartesian(angle, radius), do: apply(Geometry, :polar_to_cartesian, [angle, radius])\n116\t  defp trim_edge(a, b), do: apply(Geometry, :trim_edge, [a, b])\n117\t  defp layout(domains), do: apply(Geometry, :layout, [domains])\n118\t\n119\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n120\t  # domain_angles/1\n121\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n122\t\n123\t  describe \"domain_angles/1 — canonical 4-domain fixed angles (spec §2)\" do\n124\t    test \"the exact 4 named domains, in spec order, get the spec's fixed angles\" do\n125\t      assert domain_angles(@canonical_domains) == [\n126\t               {\"/_matrix/client\", -48.0},\n127\t               {\"/_matrix/media\", 48.0},\n128\t               {\"/admin\", 132.0},\n129\t               {\"/health\", -132.0}\n130\t             ]\n131\t    end\n132\t  end\n133\t\n134\t  describe \"domain_angles/1 — even-distribution fallback\" do\n135\t    test \"1 domain sits at -90 degrees (the fallback's own start angle)\" do\n136\t      assert domain_angles([\"/only\"]) == [{\"/only\", -90.0}]\n137\t    end\n138\t\n139\t    test \"2 domains are spaced 180 degrees apart, starting at -90\" do\n140\t      assert domain_angles([\"/a\", \"/b\"]) == [{\"/a\", -90.0}, {\"/b\", 90.0}]\n141\t    end\n142\t\n143\t    test \"3 domains are spaced 120 degrees apart, starting at -90, in input order\" do\n144\t      assert domain_angles([\"/a\", \"/b\", \"/c\"]) == [\n145\t               {\"/a\", -90.0},\n146\t               {\"/b\", 30.0},\n147\t               {\"/c\", 150.0}\n148\t             ]\n149\t    end\n150\t\n151\t    test \"the canonical 4 domain KEYS in a DIFFERENT order fall back to even distribution, not the fixed angles\" do\n152\t      out = domain_angles([\"/admin\", \"/health\", \"/_matrix/client\", \"/_matrix/media\"])\n153\t\n154\t      assert out == [\n155\t               {\"/admin\", -90.0},\n156\t               {\"/health\", 0.0},\n157\t               {\"/_matrix/client\", 90.0},\n158\t               {\"/_matrix/media\", 180.0}\n159\t             ]\n160\t    end\n161\t\n162\t    test \"5 domains (a superset of the canonical 4) also fall back to even distribution\" do\n163\t      out = domain_angles([\"/_matrix/client\", \"/_matrix/media\", \"/admin\", \"/health\", \"/extra\"])\n164\t      assert length(out) == 5\n165\t      assert Enum.map(out, &elem(&1, 1)) == [-90.0, -18.0, 54.0, 126.0, 198.0]\n166\t    end\n167\t  end\n168\t\n169\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n170\t  # satellite_angles/2\n171\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n172\t\n173\t  describe \"satellite_angles/2 — fan spread (spec §2: min(130°, n·26°))\" do\n174\t    test \"a single satellite sits exactly on the domain's own angle (no spread)\" do\n175\t      assert satellite_angles(-48.0, 1) == [-48.0]\n176\t    end\n177\t\n178\t    test \"3 satellites: spread = min(130, 78) = 78, evenly fanned\" do\n179\t      assert satellite_angles(-48.0, 3) == [-87.0, -48.0, -9.0]\n180\t    end\n181\t\n182\t    test \"6 satellites: spread caps at 130 (6 * 26 = 156 > 130)\" do\n183\t      [a1, a2, a3, a4, a5, a6] = satellite_angles(-48.0, 6)\n184\t\n185\t      assert_in_delta a1, -113.0, 0.001\n186\t      assert_in_delta a2, -87.0, 0.001\n187\t      assert_in_delta a3, -61.0, 0.001\n188\t      assert_in_delta a4, -35.0, 0.001\n189\t      assert_in_delta a5, -9.0, 0.001\n190\t      assert_in_delta a6, 17.0, 0.001\n191\t    end\n192\t\n193\t    test \"2 satellites: spread = min(130, 52) = 52, one at each edge of the spread\" do\n194\t      assert satellite_angles(0.0, 2) == [-26.0, 26.0]\n195\t    end\n196\t  end\n197\t\n198\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n199\t  # polar_to_cartesian/2\n200\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n201\t\n202\t  describe \"polar_to_cartesian/2\" do\n203\t    test \"0 degrees points along +x\" do\n204\t      {x, y} = polar_to_cartesian(0.0, 100.0)\n205\t      assert_in_delta x, 100.0, 0.0001\n206\t      assert_in_delta y, 0.0, 0.0001\n207\t    end\n208\t\n209\t    test \"90 degrees points along +y (SVG convention, no flip applied here)\" do\n210\t      {x, y} = polar_to_cartesian(90.0, 100.0)\n211\t      assert_in_delta x, 0.0, 0.0001\n212\t      assert_in_delta y, 100.0, 0.0001\n213\t    end\n214\t\n215\t    test \"matches :math.cos/:math.sin directly for an arbitrary angle\" do\n216\t      angle = -48.0\n217\t      radius = 195.0\n218\t      radians = angle * :math.pi() / 180.0\n219\t\n220\t      {x, y} = polar_to_cartesian(angle, radius)\n221\t\n222\t      assert_in_delta x, radius * :math.cos(radians), 0.0001\n223\t      assert_in_delta y, radius * :math.sin(radians), 0.0001\n224\t    end\n225\t  end\n226\t\n227\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n228\t  # trim_edge/2\n229\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n230\t\n231\t  describe \"trim_edge/2 — edges trimmed by (radius + 4px) at each end\" do\n232\t    test \"a horizontal edge from nebu (r=52) to a domain (r=38) 100 units away\" do\n233\t      nebu = %{x: 0.0, y: 0.0, r: 52}\n234\t      domain = %{x: 100.0, y: 0.0, r: 38}\n235\t\n236\t      assert trim_edge(nebu, domain) == {56.0, 0.0, 58.0, 0.0}\n237\t    end\n238\t\n239\t    test \"trimming is symmetric under endpoint swap (start/end reverse, offsets stay own-radius-relative)\" do\n240\t      a = %{x: 0.0, y: 0.0, r: 52}\n241\t      b = %{x: 100.0, y: 0.0, r: 38}\n242\t\n243\t      {x1, y1, x2, y2} = trim_edge(a, b)\n244\t      {x2r, y2r, x1r, y1r} = trim_edge(b, a)\n245\t\n246\t      assert_in_delta x1, x1r, 0.0001\n247\t      assert_in_delta y1, y1r, 0.0001\n248\t      assert_in_delta x2, x2r, 0.0001\n249\t      assert_in_delta y2, y2r, 0.0001\n250\t    end\n251\t\n252\t    test \"a diagonal edge trims along the correct unit vector\" do\n253\t      a = %{x: 0.0, y: 0.0, r: 0}\n254\t      b = %{x: 30.0, y: 40.0, r: 0}\n255\t\n256\t      # length 50, unit vector (0.6, 0.8); +4px padding at each end (r=0)\n257\t      assert {x1, y1, x2, y2} = trim_edge(a, b)\n258\t      assert_in_delta x1, 0.6 * 4, 0.0001\n259\t      assert_in_delta y1, 0.8 * 4, 0.0001\n260\t      assert_in_delta x2, 30.0 - 0.6 * 4, 0.0001\n261\t      assert_in_delta y2, 40.0 - 0.8 * 4, 0.0001\n262\t    end\n263\t  end\n264\t\n265\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n266\t  # layout/1 — full tree\n267\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n268\t\n269\t  describe \"layout/1 — nebu is always fixed at the origin\" do\n270\t    test \"nebu is {0.0, 0.0} with radius 52 regardless of domain count\" do\n271\t      layout = layout([%{key: \"/only\", satellites: [\"/only/a\"]}])\n272\t      assert layout.nebu == %{x: 0.0, y: 0.0, r: 52}\n273\t    end\n274\t  end\n275\t\n276\t  describe \"layout/1 — domains positioned on the R1=195 ring\" do\n277\t    test \"a single domain sits 195 units from the origin, at its own angle\" do\n278\t      layout = layout([%{key: \"/only\", satellites: []}])\n279\t      domain = layout.domains[\"/only\"]\n280\t\n281\t      assert domain.r == 38\n282\t      assert domain.angle == -90.0\n283\t      assert_in_delta domain.x, 195.0 * :math.cos(-90.0 * :math.pi() / 180.0), 0.001\n284\t      assert_in_delta domain.y, 195.0 * :math.sin(-90.0 * :math.pi() / 180.0), 0.001\n285\t    end\n286\t  end\n287\t\n288\t  describe \"layout/1 — satellites positioned relative to their OWN domain, on the R2=128 ring\" do\n289\t    test \"a satellite's absolute position is its domain's position plus its own polar offset\" do\n290\t      layout =\n291\t        layout([%{key: \"/only\", satellites: [\"/only/a\", \"/only/b\", \"/only/c\"]}])\n292\t\n293\t      domain = layout.domains[\"/only\"]\n294\t      sat_a = layout.satellites[\"/only/a\"]\n295\t\n296\t      assert sat_a.r == 24\n297\t      assert sat_a.domain == \"/only\"\n298\t\n299\t      [expected_angle | _] = satellite_angles(domain.angle, 3)\n300\t      {dx, dy} = polar_to_cartesian(expected_angle, 128.0)\n301\t\n302\t      assert_in_delta sat_a.x, domain.x + dx, 0.001\n303\t      assert_in_delta sat_a.y, domain.y + dy, 0.001\n304\t      assert_in_delta sat_a.angle, expected_angle, 0.001\n305\t    end\n306\t\n307\t    test \"an empty satellites list produces no satellite entries for that domain (no crash)\" do\n308\t      layout = layout([%{key: \"/empty\", satellites: []}])\n309\t      assert layout.domains[\"/empty\"]\n310\t      refute Map.has_key?(layout.satellites, \"/empty/anything\")\n311\t    end\n312\t domains = [\n317\t        %{key: \"/_matrix/client\", satellites: [\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", \"/_matrix/client/v3/login\"]},\n318\t        %{key: \"/_matrix/media\", satellites: [\"/_matrix/media/v3/upload\"]},\n319\t        %{key: \"/admin\", satellites: [\"/admin/users\"]},\n320\t        %{key: \"/health\", satellites: [\"/health\"]}\n321\t      ]\n322\t\n323\t      layout Every\n  `Nebu.Admin.TraceMapGeometry.<fun>(...)` call below fails with\n  `** (UndefinedFunctionError)`, NOT a compile error in this file (Elixir\n  does not check a remote function exists at compile time).\n\n  ## Module contract this file pins down (proposed — the dev agent may\n  restructure the public API, e.g. return structs instead of plain maps, or\n  fold these five functions into fewer/more, AS LONG AS the SVG rendered by\n  `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`'s `render/1` places every node at exactly\n  the (x, y) this contract computes — see\n  `trace_map_leitstand_test.exs`'s \"AC2: radial SVG map\" describe block for\n  the rendered-output cross-check that keeps this file honest against the\n  real template):\n\n  Constants (spec §2, viewBox `-560 -400 1120 800`):\n\n    * `@center_radius` 52 — nebu's own circle radius, always at `{0.0, 0.0}`.\n    * `@domain_radius` 38 — every domain circle's radius.\n    * `@satellite_radius` 24 — every satellite circle's radius.\n    * `@domain_ring` 195.0 — R1, the ring domains sit on (measured from nebu).\n    * `@satellite_ring` 128.0 — R2, the ring satellites sit on (measured from\n      THEIR OWN domain's center, per the dummy's `edge()`/satellite-placement\n      code — NOT from nebu).\n    * `@edge_padding` 4 — trimmed off each end of an edge line, on top of the\n      node's own radius.\n\n  Functions:\n\n    * `domain_angles(domain_keys :: [String.t()]) :: [{String.t(), float()}]`\n      — one `{domain_key, angle_degrees}` pair per input key, SAME order as\n      input (first-observed order, never re-sorted).\n\n      Canonical rule (spec §2's literal 4 named domains): when\n      `domain_keys == [\"/_matrix/client\", \"/_matrix/media\", \"/admin\",\n      \"/health\"]` EXACTLY (this order), the angles are the spec's own fixed\n      values `[-48, 48, 132, -132]` — NOT evenly spaced (132 - (-132) = 264,\n      not 90; this is a deliberately hand-placed, not a uniform, layout).\n\n      Fallback rule (spec §2: \"distribute evenly if domain count differs\" —\n      also covers the canonical 4 domains appearing in ANY OTHER order, or\n      with a 5th domain, etc.): evenly distributed starting at `-90.0`\n      degrees, stepping by `360.0 / length(domain_keys)` per domain, i.e.\n      `angle(i) = -90.0 + i * (360.0 / n)` for `i` in `0..(n - 1)`.\n\n    * `satellite_angles(domain_angle :: float(), count :: pos_integer()) ::\n      [float()]` — `count` angles fanned around `domain_angle`, spread\n      `min(130, count * 26)` degrees (spec §2's `min(130°, n·26°)`):\n\n        * `count == 1` → `[domain_angle]` (centered exactly on the domain,\n          no spread — dividing by `count - 1` would be a division by zero).\n        * `count > 1` → `domain_angle - spread / 2 + i * (spread /\n          (count - 1))` for `i` in `0..(count - 1)` (evenly fanned across\n          the full spread, first satellite at `domain_angle - spread / 2`,\n          last at `domain_angle + spread / 2`).\n\n    * `polar_to_cartesian(angle_degrees :: float(), radius :: number()) ::\n      {float(), float()}` — `{radius * :math.cos(radians), radius *\n      :math.sin(radians)}`, standard SVG convention (positive Y is down,\n      but this function does no Y-flip — same as the dummy's own `Math.cos`/\n      `Math.sin` usage).\n\n    * `trim_edge(a :: %{x: number(), y: number(), r: number()}, b :: %{x:\n      number(), y: number(), r: number()}) :: {float(), float(), float(),\n      float()}` — `{x1, y1, x2, y2}` for a straight line from `a` to `b`,\n      shortened at EACH end by that end's own `r + @edge_padding` (spec §2\n      \"node edge + 4px\"; mirrors the dummy's `edge()` function exactly).\n\n    * `layout(domains :: [%{key: String.t(), satellites: [String.t()]}]) ::\n      %{nebu: map(), domains: %{String.t() => map()}, satellites:\n      %{String.t() => map()}}` — the full computed tree: `nebu` is always\n      `%{x: 0.0, y: 0.0, r: 52}`; each domain map has `:x`/`:y`/`:r`/\n      `:angle`; each satellite map has `:x`/`:y`/`:r`/`:angle`/`:domain`\n      (its parent's key) — satellite absolute position is the PARENT\n      DOMAIN's position plus the satellite's own polar offset (see\n      `satellite_ring`'s doc above), not an offset from the origin.\n  \"\"\"\n\n  use ExUnit.Case, async: true\n\n  alias Nebu.Admin.TraceMapGeometry, as: Geometry\n\n  @canonical_domains [\"/_matrix/client\", \"/_matrix/media\", \"/admin\", \"/health\"]\n\n  # `apply/3` (not a direct `Geometry.foo(...)` remote call) is used for\n  # EVERY call into the not-yet-existing `Nebu.Admin.TraceMapGeometry`\n  # below. `mix test --warnings-as-errors` (make test-unit-elixir) runs\n  # `mix xref`-backed \"undefined remote function\" checks on ordinary direct\n  # calls to a module known not to define that function anywhere in this\n  # project — which would turn this file's entire RED-phase INTENT (fail at\n  # runtime, not fail to compile) into a hard compile error instead. This is\n  # a genuinely different situation from this repo's existing\n  # `Phoenix.Component`/LiveView ATDD precedents (`trace_map_bubble_test.exs`'s\n  # `render_component(&Mod.fun/1, ...)` capture, `logs_live_test.exs`'s\n  # `live_isolated(conn, Mod, ...)` module-atom-as-data) — `Geometry` is a\n  # PLAIN module with no such indirection available, so `apply/3` (the\n  # standard, fully-dynamic escape hatch `mix xref` cannot see through) is\n  # the correct RED-phase-safe equivalent for a plain-function module\n  # contract like this one.\n  defp domain_angles(keys), do: apply(Geometry, :domain_angles, [keys])\n  defp satellite_angles(angle, count), do: apply(Geometry, :satellite_angles, [angle, count])\n  defp polar_to_cartesian(angle, radius), do: apply(Geometry, :polar_to_cartesian, [angle, radius])\n  defp trim_edge(a, b), do: apply(Geometry, :trim_edge, [a, b])\n  defp layout(domains), do: apply(Geometry, :layout, [domains])\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # domain_angles/1\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"domain_angles/1 — canonical 4-domain fixed angles (spec §2)\" do\n    test \"the exact 4 named domains, in spec order, get the spec's fixed angles\" do\n      assert domain_angles(@canonical_domains) == [\n               {\"/_matrix/client\", -48.0},\n               {\"/_matrix/media\", 48.0},\n               {\"/admin\", 132.0},\n               {\"/health\", -132.0}\n             ]\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"domain_angles/1 — even-distribution fallback\" do\n    test \"1 domain sits at -90 degrees (the fallback's own start angle)\" do\n      assert domain_angles([\"/only\"]) == [{\"/only\", -90.0}]\n    end\n\n    test \"2 domains are spaced 180 degrees apart, starting at -90\" do\n      assert domain_angles([\"/a\", \"/b\"]) == [{\"/a\", -90.0}, {\"/b\", 90.0}]\n    end\n\n    test \"3 domains are spaced 120 degrees apart, starting at -90, in input order\" do\n      assert domain_angles([\"/a\", \"/b\", \"/c\"]) == [\n               {\"/a\", -90.0},\n               {\"/b\", 30.0},\n               {\"/c\", 150.0}\n             ]\n    end\n\n    test \"the canonical 4 domain KEYS in a DIFFERENT order fall back to even distribution, not the fixed angles\" do\n      out = domain_angles([\"/admin\", \"/health\", \"/_matrix/client\", \"/_matrix/media\"])\n\n      assert out == [\n               {\"/admin\", -90.0},\n               {\"/health\", 0.0},\n               {\"/_matrix/client\", 90.0},\n               {\"/_matrix/media\", 180.0}\n             ]\n    end\n\n    test \"5 domains (a superset of the canonical 4) also fall back to even distribution\" do\n      out = domain_angles([\"/_matrix/client\", \"/_matrix/media\", \"/admin\", \"/health\", \"/extra\"])\n      assert length(out) == 5\n      assert Enum.map(out, &elem(&1, 1)) == [-90.0, -18.0, 54.0, 126.0, 198.0]\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # satellite_angles/2\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"satellite_angles/2 — fan spread (spec §2: min(130°, n·26°))\" do\n    test \"a single satellite sits exactly on the domain's own angle (no spread)\" do\n      assert satellite_angles(-48.0, 1) == [-48.0]\n    end\n\n    test \"3 satellites: spread = min(130, 78) = 78, evenly fanned\" do\n      assert satellite_angles(-48.0, 3) == [-87.0, -48.0, -9.0]\n    end\n\n    test \"6 satellites: spread caps at 130 (6 * 26 = 156 > 130)\" do\n      [a1, a2, a3, a4, a5, a6] = satellite_angles(-48.0, 6)\n\n      assert_in_delta a1, -113.0, 0.001\n      assert_in_delta a2, -87.0, 0.001\n      assert_in_delta a3, -61.0, 0.001\n      assert_in_delta a4, -35.0, 0.001\n      assert_in_delta a5, -9.0, 0.001\n      assert_in_delta a6, 17.0, 0.001\n    end\n\n    test \"2 satellites: spread = min(130, 52) = 52, one at each edge of the spread\" do\n      assert satellite_angles(0.0, 2) == [-26.0, 26.0]\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # polar_to_cartesian/2\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"polar_to_cartesian/2\" do\n    test \"0 degrees points along +x\" do\n      {x, y} = polar_to_cartesian(0.0, 100.0)\n      assert_in_delta x, 100.0, 0.0001\n      assert_in_delta y, 0.0, 0.0001\n    end\n\n    test \"90 degrees points along +y (SVG convention, no flip applied here)\" do\n      {x, y} = polar_to_cartesian(90.0, 100.0)\n      assert_in_delta x, 0.0, 0.0001\n      assert_in_delta y, 100.0, 0.0001\n    end\n\n    test \"matches :math.cos/:math.sin directly for an arbitrary angle\" do\n      angle = -48.0\n      radius = 195.0\n      radians = angle * :math.pi() / 180.0\n\n      {x, y} = polar_to_cartesian(angle, radius)\n\n      assert_in_delta x, radius * :math.cos(radians), 0.0001\n      assert_in_delta y, radius * :math.sin(radians), 0.0001\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # trim_edge/2\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"trim_edge/2 — edges trimmed by (radius + 4px) at each end\" do\n    test \"a horizontal edge from nebu (r=52) to a domain (r=38) 100 units away\" do\n      nebu = %{x: 0.0, y: 0.0, r: 52}\n      domain = %{x: 100.0, y: 0.0, r: 38}\n\n      assert trim_edge(nebu, domain) == {56.0, 0.0, 58.0, 0.0}\n    end\n\n    test \"trimming is symmetric under endpoint swap (start/end reverse, offsets stay own-radius-relative)\" do\n      a = %{x: 0.0, y: 0.0, r: 52}\n      b = %{x: 100.0, y: 0.0, r: 38}\n\n      {x1, y1, x2, y2} = trim_edge(a, b)\n      {x2r, y2r, x1r, y1r} = trim_edge(b, a)\n\n      assert_in_delta x1, x1r, 0.0001\n      assert_in_delta y1, y1r, 0.0001\n      assert_in_delta x2, x2r, 0.0001\n      assert_in_delta y2, y2r, 0.0001\n    end\n\n    test \"a diagonal edge trims along the correct unit vector\" do\n      a = %{x: 0.0, y: 0.0, r: 0}\n      b = %{x: 30.0, y: 40.0, r: 0}\n\n      # length 50, unit vector (0.6, 0.8); +4px padding at each end (r=0)\n      assert {x1, y1, x2, y2} = trim_edge(a, b)\n      assert_in_delta x1, 0.6 * 4, 0.0001\n      assert_in_delta y1, 0.8 * 4, 0.0001\n      assert_in_delta x2, 30.0 - 0.6 * 4, 0.0001\n      assert_in_delta y2, 40.0 - 0.8 * 4, 0.0001\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # layout/1 — full tree\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"layout/1 — nebu is always fixed at the origin\" do\n    test \"nebu is {0.0, 0.0} with radius 52 regardless of domain count\" do\n      layout = layout([%{key: \"/only\", satellites: [\"/only/a\"]}])\n      assert layout.nebu == %{x: 0.0, y: 0.0, r: 52}\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"layout/1 — domains positioned on the R1=195 ring\" do\n    test \"a single domain sits 195 units from the origin, at its own angle\" do\n      layout = layout([%{key: \"/only\", satellites: []}])\n      domain = layout.domains[\"/only\"]\n\n      assert domain.r == 38\n      assert domain.angle == -90.0\n      assert_in_delta domain.x, 195.0 * :math.cos(-90.0 * :math.pi() / 180.0), 0.001\n      assert_in_delta domain.y, 195.0 * :math.sin(-90.0 * :math.pi() / 180.0), 0.001\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"layout/1 — satellites positioned relative to their OWN domain, on the R2=128 ring\" do\n    test \"a satellite's absolute position is its domain's position plus its own polar offset\" do\n      layout =\n        layout([%{key: \"/only\", satellites: [\"/only/a\", \"/only/b\", \"/only/c\"]}])\n\n      domain = layout.domains[\"/only\"]\n      sat_a = layout.satellites[\"/only/a\"]\n\n      assert sat_a.r == 24\n      assert sat_a.domain == \"/only\"\n\n      [expected_angle | _] = satellite_angles(domain.angle, 3)\n      {dx, dy} = polar_to_cartesian(expected_angle, 128.0)\n\n      assert_in_delta sat_a.x, domain.x + dx, 0.001\n      assert_in_delta sat_a.y, domain.y + dy, 0.001\n      assert_in_delta sat_a.angle, expected_angle, 0.001\n    end\n\n    test \"an empty satellites list produces no satellite entries for that domain (no crash)\" do\n      layout = layout([%{key: \"/empty\", satellites: []}])\n      assert layout.domains[\"/empty\"]\n      refute Map.has_key?(layout.satellites, \"/empty/anything\")\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"layout/1 — the canonical 4-domain shape end-to-end\" do\n    test \"all 4 domains and their satellites are present, keyed by their own strings\" do\n      domains = [\n        %{key: \"/_matrix/client\", satellites: [\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", \"/_matrix/client/v3/login\"]},\n        %{key: \"/_matrix/media\", satellites: [\"/_matrix/media/v3/upload\"]},\n        %{key: \"/admin\", satellites: [\"/admin/users\"]},\n        %{key: \"/health\", satellites: [\"/health\"]}\n      ]\n\n      layout = layout(domains)\n\n      assert Map.keys(layout.domains) |> Enum.sort() ==\n               Enum.sort(@canonical_domains)\n\n      assert layout.domains[\"/_matrix/client\"].angle == -48.0\n      assert layout.domains[\"/health\"].angle == -132.0\n\n      assert layout.satellites[\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\"].domain == \"/_matrix/client\"\n      assert layout.satellites[\"/health\"].domain == \"/health\"\n    end\n  end\nend\n","numLines":336,"startLine":1,"totalLines":336}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"aa8440e4-1cc6-4e4b-b0f6-7ddb5e83d17e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

Every call below\n14\t  fails with `** (UndefinedFunctionError)`, NOT a compile error in this file.\n15\t\n16\t  ## Module contract this file pins down (proposed plain module — the dev\n17\t  agent's own Dev Notes, \"Known Challenges\" item 1, name this exact\n18\t  Option A design: \"a rolling buffer of `{timestamp, level}` tuples per\n19\t  node\" — this file concretizes that into a testable API. The dev agent may\n20\t  restructure the internal representation (e.g. a single tagged list\n21\t  instead of three separate lists) as long as `counts/2`/`status/2`/\n22\t  `worst/1`'s OBSERVABLE behavior below holds; `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`\n23\t  is expected to keep one of these states per map node (nebu, each domain,\n24\t  each satellite) in a socket assign, e.g. `%{node_key => state}`):\n25\t\n26\t    * `new/0 :: state` — an empty node state (no events recorded yet).\n27\t\n28\t    * `record(state, kind, timestamp_ms) :: state` where `kind` is one of\n29\t      `:request | :warning | :error` — appends one event of that kind at\n30\t      `timestamp_ms` (a plain integer millisecond timestamp — NOT a\n31\t      `DateTime`, so tests can drive the window boundary with exact,\n32\t      deterministic integers instead of fighting `DateTime` arithmetic/\n33\t      flakiness; `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap` converts real timestamps to\n34\t      `System.system_time(:millisecond)` or equivalent before calling this).\n35\t\n36\t    * `counts(state, now_ms) :: %{requests: non_neg_integer(), warnings:\n37\t      non_neg_integer(), errors: non_neg_integer()}` — counts of events of\n38\t      each kind still inside the 60-SECOND (60_000 ms) window ending at\n39\t      `now_ms`, i.e. events with `now_ms - timestamp_ms < 60_000`. This\n40\t      function is PURE (does not mutate/return a pruned `state` — every\n41\t      call independently filters by `now_ms`, so there is no risk of\n42\t      \"forgot to prune before reading\" bugs).\n43\t\n44\t      **Deliberate departure from the sibling `admin-logs-monitoring-view`\n45\t      page's Trace Map bubbles**: THOSE bubble counts (`@endpoint_counts`\n46\t      etc. in `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`) are cumulative SINCE PAGE LOAD, never\n47\t      windowed. AC3 is explicit that the Leitstand's counts — INCLUDING the\n48\t      request count, not just warnings/errors — are a rolling 60-second\n49\t      window: \"Each node tracks request count, warning count, error count\n50\t      from entries in `LogStore` where `inserted_at > now() - 60 seconds`\".\n51\t      A dev tempted to reuse the sibling page's cumulative-counter pattern\n52\t      for `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap` would silently violate AC3 — see\n53\t      the \"requests are ALSO windowed\" test below, which exists\n54\t      specifically to catch that mistake.\n55\t\n56\t    * `status(state, now_ms) :: :ok | :warn | :error` — derived from\n57\t      `counts/2` at `now_ms`: `errors > 0` → `:error`; else `warnings > 0`\n58\t      → `:warn`; else `:ok` (spec §2's literal rule).\n59\t\n60\t    * `worst(statuses :: [:ok | :warn | :error]) :: :ok | :warn | :error` —\n61\t      worst-child propagation (spec §2: \"a parent's status is the worst of\n62\t      its children\"): precedence `:error` > `:warn` > `:ok`. An empty list\n63\t      (a domain with no satellites, or nebu with no domains — degenerate,\n64\t      but must not crash) is `:ok`.\n65\t\n66\t  ## Auto-heal (spec §2: \"Nodes heal automatically when the window passes —\n67\t  no manual acknowledge\")\n68\t\n69\t  There is deliberately NO `heal/1`, `ack/1`, or any other explicit\n70\t  \"clear this node's alarm\" function in this contract. Auto-heal is not a\n71\t  distinct operation — it falls directly out of `counts/2`/`status/2` being\n72\t  pure functions of `(state, now_ms)`: the SAME state, queried at a LATER\n73\t  `now_ms` once every event has aged out of the 60-second window, is `:ok`\n74\t  with zero counts, with no code path that \"forgets\" to re-evaluate it.\n75\t  \"\"\"\n76\t\n77\t  use ExUnit.Case, async: true\n78\t\n79\t  alias Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapStatus, as: Status\n80\t\n81\t  @window_ms 60_000\n82\t\n83\t  # `apply/3` (not a direct `Status.foo(...)` remote call) is used for every\n84\t  # call into the not-yet-existing `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapStatus`\n85\t  # below — see `trace_map_geometry_test.exs`'s identical wrapper-functions\n86\t  # comment for the full rationale (`mix test --warnings-as-errors`'s\n87\t  # `mix xref` undefined-remote-function check would otherwise turn this\n88\t  # file's RED-phase INTENT — fail at runtime — into a hard compile error).\n89\t  defp new_status, do: apply(Status, :new, [])\n90\t  defp record(state, kind, ts), do: apply(Status, :record, [state, kind, ts])\n91\t  defp counts(state, now), do: apply(Status, :counts, [state, now])\n92\t  defp status(state, now), do: apply(Status, :status, [state, now])\n93\t  defp worst(statuses), do: apply(Status, :worst, [statuses])\n94\t\n95\t  # Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapStatusTest do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  RED-phase ATDD tests for `enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand`, AC3\n  (status model — 60-second sliding window, worst-child propagation,\n  auto-heal).\n\n  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand.md\n  Design: _bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-spec.md §2\n\n  ## Why this MUST FAIL right now\n\n  `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapStatus` does not exist yet — none of AC3's\n  window/propagation/auto-heal logic has been written. Every call below\n  fails with `** (UndefinedFunctionError)`, NOT a compile error in this file.\n\n  ## Module contract this file pins down (proposed plain module — the dev\n  agent's own Dev Notes, \"Known Challenges\" item 1, name this exact\n  Option A design: \"a rolling buffer of `{timestamp, level}` tuples per\n  node\" — this file concretizes that into a testable API. The dev agent may\n  restructure the internal representation (e.g. a single tagged list\n  instead of three separate lists) as long as `counts/2`/`status/2`/\n  `worst/1`'s OBSERVABLE behavior below holds; `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`\n  is expected to keep one of these states per map node (nebu, each domain,\n  each satellite) in a socket assign, e.g. `%{node_key => state}`):\n\n    * `new/0 :: state` — an empty node state (no events recorded yet).\n\n    * `record(state, kind, timestamp_ms) :: state` where `kind` is one of\n      `:request | :warning | :error` — appends one event of that kind at\n      `timestamp_ms` (a plain integer millisecond timestamp — NOT a\n      `DateTime`, so tests can drive the window boundary with exact,\n      deterministic integers instead of fighting `DateTime` arithmetic/\n      flakiness; `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap` converts real timestamps to\n      `System.system_time(:millisecond)` or equivalent before calling this).\n\n    * `counts(state, now_ms) :: %{requests: non_neg_integer(), warnings:\n      non_neg_integer(), errors: non_neg_integer()}` — counts of events of\n      each kind still inside the 60-SECOND (60_000 ms) window ending at\n      `now_ms`, i.e. events with `now_ms - timestamp_ms < 60_000`. This\n      function is PURE (does not mutate/return a pruned `state` — every\n      call independently filters by `now_ms`, so there is no risk of\n      \"forgot to prune before reading\" bugs).\n\n      **Deliberate departure from the sibling `admin-logs-monitoring-view`\n      page's Trace Map bubbles**: THOSE bubble counts (`@endpoint_counts`\n      etc. in `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`) are cumulative SINCE PAGE LOAD, never\n      windowed. AC3 is explicit that the Leitstand's counts — INCLUDING the\n      request count, not just warnings/errors — are a rolling 60-second\n      window: \"Each node tracks request count, warning count, error count\n      from entries in `LogStore` where `inserted_at > now() - 60 seconds`\".\n      A dev tempted to reuse the sibling page's cumulative-counter pattern\n      for `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap` would silently violate AC3 — see\n      the \"requests are ALSO windowed\" test below, which exists\n      specifically to catch that mistake.\n\n    * `status(state, now_ms) :: :ok | :warn | :error` — derived from\n      `counts/2` at `now_ms`: `errors > 0` → `:error`; else `warnings > 0`\n      → `:warn`; else `:ok` (spec §2's literal rule).\n\n    * `worst(statuses :: [:ok | :warn | :error]) :: :ok | :warn | :error` —\n      worst-child propagation (spec §2: \"a parent's status is the worst of\n      its children\"): precedence `:error` > `:warn` > `:ok`. An empty list\n      (a domain with no satellites, or nebu with no domains — degenerate,\n      but must not crash) is `:ok`.\n\n  ## Auto-heal (spec §2: \"Nodes heal automatically when the window passes —\n  no manual acknowledge\")\n\n  There is deliberately NO `heal/1`, `ack/1`, or any other explicit\n  \"clear this node's alarm\" function in this contract. Auto-heal is not a\n  distinct operation — it falls directly out of `counts/2`/`status/2` being\n  pure functions of `(state, now_ms)`: the SAME state, queried at a LATER\n  `now_ms` once every event has aged out of the 60-second window, is `:ok`\n  with zero counts, with no code path that \"forgets\" to re-evaluate it.\n  \"\"\"\n\n  use ExUnit.Case, async: true\n\n  alias Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapStatus, as: Status\n\n  @window_ms 60_000\n\n  # `apply/3` (not a direct `Status.foo(...)` remote call) is used for every\n  # call into the not-yet-existing `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapStatus`\n  # below — see `trace_map_geometry_test.exs`'s identical wrapper-functions\n  # comment for the full rationale (`mix test --warnings-as-errors`'s\n  # `mix xref` undefined-remote-function check would otherwise turn this\n  # file's RED-phase INTENT — fail at runtime — into a hard compile error).\n  defp new_status, do: apply(Status, :new, [])\n  defp record(state, kind, ts), do: apply(Status, :record, [state, kind, ts])\n  defp counts(state, now), do: apply(Status, :counts, [state, now])\n  defp status(state, now), do: apply(Status, :status, [state, now])\n  defp worst(statuses), do: apply(Status, :worst, [statuses])\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # new/0 + record/3 + counts/2 — basic bookkeeping\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"new/0 — an empty node has zero counts and :ok status\" do\n    test \"counts/2 are all zero\" do\n      state = new_status()\n      assert counts(state, 0) == %{requests: 0, warnings: 0, errors: 0}\n    end\n\n    test \"status/2 is :ok\" do\n      assert status(new_status(), 0) == :ok\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"record/3 — appends one event of the given kind\" do\n    test \"a :request increments the requests count, leaving warnings/errors at zero\" do\n      state = new_status() |> record(:request, 0)\n      assert counts(state, 0) == %{requests: 1, warnings: 0, errors: 0}\n    end\n\n    test \"recording 3 requests, 2 warnings, 1 error at the same timestamp counts each independently\" do\n      state =\n        new_status()\n        |> record(:request, 1_000)\n        |> record(:request, 1_000)\n        |> record(:request, 1_000)\n        |> record(:warning, 1_000)\n        |> record(:warning, 1_000)\n        |> record(:error, 1_000)\n\n      assert counts(state, 1_000) == %{requests: 3, warnings: 2, errors: 1}\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # status/2 — error > 0 -> :error, else warn > 0 -> :warn, else :ok\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"status/2 — precedence rule (spec §2)\" do\n    test \"any error present -> :error, even alongside warnings\" do\n      state = new_status() |> record(:warning, 0) |> record(:error, 0)\n      assert status(state, 0) == :error\n    end\n\n    test \"warnings but no errors -> :warn\" do\n      state = new_status() |> record(:warning, 0)\n      assert status(state, 0) == :warn\n    end\n\n    test \"only requests, no warnings/errors -> :ok\" do\n      state = new_status() |> record(:request, 0) |> record(:request, 0)\n      assert status(state, 0) == :ok\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # 60-second sliding window + auto-heal\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"60-second sliding window (spec §2) — auto-heal with no explicit acknowledge\" do\n    test \"a warning is still counted 1ms before the window closes\" do\n      state = new_status() |> record(:warning, 0)\n      assert status(state, @window_ms - 1) == :warn\n      assert counts(state, @window_ms - 1).warnings == 1\n    end\n\n    test \"a warning ages out exactly at the 60s boundary and the node auto-heals to :ok\" do\n      state = new_status() |> record(:warning, 0)\n\n      assert status(state, @window_ms) == :ok\n      assert counts(state, @window_ms) == %{requests: 0, warnings: 0, errors: 0}\n    end\n\n    test \"an error ages out the same way, with no manual acknowledge call anywhere in this test\" do\n      state = new_status() |> record(:error, 0)\n\n      assert status(state, 30_000) == :error\n      assert status(state, @window_ms + 1) == :ok\n    end\n\n    test \"requests are ALSO windowed — NOT cumulative like the sibling page's bubble counts (AC3, see moduledoc)\" do\n      state =\n        new_status()\n        |> record(:request, 0)\n        |> record(:request, 0)\n        |> record(:request, 0)\n\n      assert counts(state, 0).requests == 3\n      assert counts(state, @window_ms + 1).requests == 0\n    end\n\n    test \"independent aging: an earlier warning heals while a later error in the same node stays active\" do\n      state =\n        new_status()\n        |> record(:warning, 0)\n        |> record(:error, 30_000)\n\n      # At t=61_000: warning (recorded at 0) is 61_000ms old -> aged out.\n      #              error (recorded at 30_000) is 31_000ms old -> still active.\n      status_at_61s = status(state, 61_000)\n      counts_at_61s = counts(state, 61_000)\n\n      assert status_at_61s == :error\n      assert counts_at_61s.warnings == 0\n      assert counts_at_61s.errors == 1\n\n      # At t=95_000: the error (recorded at 30_000) is now 65_000ms old -> heals too.\n      assert status(state, 95_000) == :ok\n    end\n\n    test \"a fresh event after healing re-activates the node (the window is a rolling function of `now`, not a one-shot latch)\" do\n      state = new_status() |> record(:error, 0)\n\n      assert status(state, @window_ms + 1) == :ok\n\n      state = record(state, :error, @window_ms + 1)\n      assert status(state, @window_ms + 1) == :error\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # worst/1 — worst-child propagation (satellite -> domain -> nebu)\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"worst/1 — worst-child propagation (spec §2)\" do\n    test \"an empty list of children (a domain with no satellites) is :ok, never crashes\" do\n      assert worst([]) == :ok\n    end\n\n    test \"all-:ok children -> :ok\" do\n      assert worst([:ok, :ok, :ok]) == :ok\n    end\n\n    test \"one :warn among :ok children -> :warn\" do\n      assert worst([:ok, :warn, :ok]) == :warn\n    end\n\n    test \"one :error among :ok and :warn children -> :error (error beats warn)\" do\n      assert worst([:ok, :warn, :error, :ok]) == :error\n    end\n\n    test \"propagation composes: a domain's worst-of-satellites feeds into nebu's worst-of-domains\" do\n      domain_a_status = worst([:ok, :ok])\n      domain_b_status = worst([:warn, :ok])\n      domain_c_status = worst([:error, :warn])\n\n      assert domain_a_status == :ok\n      assert domain_b_status == :warn\n      assert domain_c_status == :error\n\n      nebu_status = worst([domain_a_status, domain_b_status, domain_c_status])\n      assert nebu_status == :error\n    end\n  end\nend\n","numLines":251,"startLine":1,"totalLines":251}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e4ba01af-e0f8-46df-bb4a-e6b47711233b","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

Every test\n20\t  below fails at `live/1` with either a 404/redirect (stub route still\n21\t  serves the old page) or `UndefinedFunctionError` once the route is\n22\t  updated but the module is still missing — NOT a compile error in this\n23\t  file.\n24\t\n25\t  ## Architecture decision this file pins down: a SEPARATE LiveView module,\n26\t  not a new action on the existing `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`\n27\t\n28\t  AC1's literal text says \"the LiveView module `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive` (or\n29\t  alias) is extended with a new `mount/3` action for trace-map\". This file\n30\t  deliberately pins the \"(or alias)\" branch — a NEW, SEPARATE module\n31\t  `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap` — for a concrete, load-bearing reason\n32\t  already documented in the EXISTING `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s own moduledoc\n33\t  (see `logs_live.ex`, \"Trace Map filter toggle is server-tracked\" section):\n34\t  merely EXPORTING a `handle_params/3` callback on a LiveView module breaks\n35\t  EVERY `live_isolated/3`-mounted test in that module's own suite (empirically\n36\t  verified against the real, installed `phoenix_live_view` dependency —\n37\t  `live_isolated/3` unconditionally rejects `handle_params/3` regardless of\n38\t  whether a patch is ever attempted). AC5 HARD-REQUIRES `handle_params/3`\n39\t  for this new page (the whole point of the story, per the predecessor\n40\t  `trace-map-filter-reset-bugfix`'s lesson: URL-driven state, not\n41\t  client-DOM-only). Adding `handle_params/3` to the EXISTING, shared\n42\t  `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive` module would therefore break all ~200 pre-existing\n43\t  `live_isolated/3`-based tests in `logs_live_test.exs` for the UNRELATED\n44\t  `:index` (Live Stream) action — a story-scope-violating regression this\n45\t  file's own architecture avoids entirely by giving the Leitstand page its\n46\t  own module. The dev agent may still choose a different module name/split\n47\t  IF the rendered markup and the router contract below hold; the LOAD-BEARING\n48\t  constraint is \"not the same module as the existing `:index` action\".\n49\t\n50\t  Router contract (replaces the existing stub in `router.ex`):\n51\t\n52\t      live \"/logs/trace-map\", LogsLive.TraceMap, :index\n53\t\n54\t  `@active_nav` is set to `\"logs-trace-map\"` in `mount/3`.\n55\t\n56\t  ## Why (almost) every test below is router-mounted, not `live_isolated/3`\n57\t\n58\t  Per the architecture decision above, `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap` DOES\n59\t  define `handle_params/3` — so `live_isolated/3` cannot mount it at all\n60\t  (see above). Every test therefore dispatches through the REAL router via\n61\t  `Phoenix.LiveViewTest.live/1` + a real, SENT conn (mirrors\n62\t  `logs_live_test.exs`'s own \"AC12: mount/3's war_room_id param\" describe\n63\t  block's `admin_conn_for_live/1` helper EXACTLY). Despite being\n64\t  router-mounted, MOST tests below still run under plain `mix test`\n65\t  (`make test-unit-elixir`, no `Nebu.Repo`) because:\n66\t\n67\t    * `Nebu.Admin.Auth.bootstrap_completed?/0` defaults to `true` when\n68\t      neither the `:admin_bootstrap_store` seam nor `Nebu.Repo` is running\n69\t      (see `auth.ex`'s own doc comment) — the `on_mount(:require_admin)`\n70\t      gate does not redirect these tests to `/admin/bootstrap`.\n71\t    * `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`'s `mount/3` MUST guard every DB read\n72\t      (initial endpoint/domain history, war-room status) behind\n73\t      `Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo)`, mirroring the EXISTING `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s\n74\t      own `initial_entries/1`/`initial_war_room_status/0` precedent — this\n75\t      is a REQUIRED contract, not optional, precisely so this file's tests\n76\t      run DB-free.\n77\t    * `Nebu.PubSub` (unlike `Nebu.Repo`) IS running in the plain unit-test\n78\t      environment (confirmed by the existing `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s own\n79\t      `mount/3` unconditionally subscribing to it, exercised by hundreds of\n80\t      already-passing `live_isolated/3` tests) — so tests can drive live\n81\t      status updates via a real `Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast/3` on the SAME\n82\t      `\"operational_logs\"` topic the existing page already uses, with no\n83\t      DB involved.\n84\t\n85\t  The ONE describe block that genuinely needs a live session/DB (the\n86\t  war-room banner's ACTIVE state) is tagged `@describetag :integration` and\n87\t  mirrors the existing `admin_conn_for_live/1` + `Nebu.Release.migrate/0` +\n88\t  raw-SQL-seed pattern from `logs_live_test.exs`'s AC12 block — it will not\n89\t  run under `make test-unit-elixir`, only `make test-integration`, exactly\n90\t  like that existing precedent.\n91\t\n92\t  ## Correction: AC5/AC12's literal repeated bare `?endpoint=X&endpoint=Y`\n93\t  URL format does not survive `Plug.Conn.Query.decode/1`\n94\t\n95\t  AC5/AC12 Scenario 10 both show a bookmarked URL as\n96\t  `?endpoint=%2Fsync&endpoint=%2Fsend` — repeated BARE (non-bracket) query\n97\t  keys. `Plug.Conn.Query.decode/1` documents \"if a value is given more than\n98\t  once, the LAST value takes precedence\" for a bare repeated key (the\n99\t  EXACT same footgun `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s own `active_filters_from_params/2`\n100\t  comment already flags, which is why that sibling page comma-joins a\n101\t  single value instead). This story's AC5, unlike the sibling page, does\n102\t  NOT propose comma-joining — so this file instead pins the\n103\t  BRACKET-ARRAY query format `?endpoint[]=X&endpoint[]=Y`, which\n104\t  `Plug.Conn.Query.decode/1` natively decodes into `%{\"endpoint\" => [\"X\",\n105\t  \"Y\"]}` with no data loss and no custom parsing. This is a genuine\n106\t  discrepancy between the story's literal example and Plug's real decoding\n107\t  behavior — flagged here (and in the ATDD handoff report) for the dev/PM\n108\t  to explicitly confirm; the dev agent may instead hand-parse\n109\t  `conn.query_string` directly to honor the story's literal bare-repeated\n110\t  format IF the rendered `handle_params/3` behavior below (multi-select\n111\t  round-trips correctly) still holds.\n112\t\n113\t  ## Data-attribute / event contract this file pins down\n114\t\n115\t    * SVG root: `[data-testid=\"trace-map-svg\"]`, `viewBox=\"-560 -400 1120 800\"`.\n116\t    * Every node (nebu/domain/satellite): `[data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"]\n117\t      [data-node-key=...] [data-node-kind=\"nebu\"|\"domain\"|\"satellite\"]\n118\t      [data-node-status=\"ok\"|\"warn\"|\"error\"] [data-node-selected=\"true\"|\"false\"]\n119\t      role=\"button\" tabindex=\"0\" aria-pressed=\"true\"|\"false\" aria-label=\"...\".`\n120\t      Nebu's own `aria-pressed` is always `\"false\"` (it is not itself a\n121\t      toggle target — clicking it CLEARS filters instead, spec §2/AC5).\n122\t    * Click contract: non-nebu nodes carry `phx-click=\"toggle_node\"\n123\t      phx-value-key={key}`; nebu carries `phx-click=\"clear_filters\"`. Unlike\n124\t      the sibling `admin-logs-monitoring-view` page's Trace Map bubbles,\n125\t      this is an ORDINARY server round-trip (no AC3-style\n126\t      client-side-only/zero-lag constraint applies to this story) — a plain\n127\t      `phx-click` event name, NOT a `Phoenix.LiveView.JS` command list.\n128\t    * Filter chips: `[data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip\"]\n129\t      [data-chip-value=...]`, each containing\n130\t      `[data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip-remove\"]` (same `toggle_node`\n131\t      handler, removing = toggling off); `[data-testid=\"trace-map-clear-all\"]`\n132\t      (`phx-click=\"clear_filters\"`).\n133\t    * War-room banner: `[data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-banner\"]`, `hidden`\n134\t      attribute present when inactive, `role=\"status\" aria-live=\"polite\"`;\n135\t      recording dot `[data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-dot\"]`; open-link\n136\t      `[data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-open-link\"]` navigating to\n137\t      `/admin/logs/war-room`.\n138\t    * Tooltip data (AC7 — content only; show/hide on hover/focus is\n139\t      app.js/E2E territory): every node ALSO carries `data-tooltip-name`,\n140\t      `data-tooltip-route`, `data-tooltip-status`, `data-tooltip-counts`,\n141\t      `data-tooltip-cta` attributes with the values a hover/focus JS hook\n142\t      would render into the shared `[data-testid=\"trace-map-tooltip\"]`\n143\t      element — no payload/token/message content, per spec §8's\n144\t      no-sensitive-data constraint.\n145\t    * Inspector: `[data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector\"]`, empty state\n146\t      `[data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-empty\"]`; once a node has been\n147\t      clicked/inspected: `[data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-name\"]`,\n148\t      `-route`, `-status-pill][data-status=...]`, `-stat-requests`,\n149\t      `-stat-warnings`, `-stat-errors`, and one\n150\t      `[data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-event\"]` per recent event (newest\n151\t      first, capped at 5).\n152\t    * Legend: `[data-testid=\"trace-map-legend\"]` containing exactly 6\n153\t      `[data-testid=\"trace-map-legend-row\"][data-legend-state=...]` rows\n154\t      (`ok`, `warn`, `error`, `selected`, `off`, `temp`) — ALWAYS rendered,\n155\t      regardless of what is currently on the map (spec §2's \"always\n156\t      visible\" legend).\n157\t    * Stream panel: `[data-testid=\"trace-map-stream\"]`; log lines\n158\t      `[data-testid=\"trace-map-stream-line\"][data-level=...][data-route=...]`;\n159\t      level pills `[data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"DEBUG\"|\n160\t      \"INFO\"|\"WARN\"|\"ERROR\"]` `aria-pressed`, `phx-click=\"toggle_level\"\n161\t      phx-value-level={level}` — internally mapped to the lowercase\n162\t      `Nebu.Web.LogStore` level strings via `%{\"DEBUG\" => \"debug\", \"INFO\" =>\n163\t      \"info\", \"WARN\" => \"warning\", \"ERROR\" => \"error\"}` (the pill vocabulary\n164\t      is the spec's own abbreviated `WARN`/`ERROR`; `LogStore` entries use\n165\t      `\"warning\"`/`\"error\"` — this mapping is the dev agent's own\n166\t      responsibility to get right, pinned here explicitly since it is an\n167\t      easy silent-mismatch trap).\n168\t    * Resizer: `[data-testid=\"trace-map-resizer\"] role=\"separator\"\n169\t      aria-orientation=\"horizontal\" tabindex=\"0\" aria-label=...` — actual\n170\t      pointer-drag/keyboard resize behavior is CSS/JS-driven and only\n171\t      testable in a real browser (`logs-trace-map.feature` Scenario 9);\n172\t      this file only pins the static, accessible markup contract.\n173\t    * Live announcer: `[data-testid=\"trace-map-announcer\"] role=\"status\"\n174\t      aria-live=\"polite\"`.\n175\t  \"\"\"\n176\t\n177\t  use ExUnit.Case, async: false\n178\t\n179\t  import Plug.Test\n180\t  import Plug.Conn\n181\t  import Phoenix.LiveViewTest\n182\t  # `Phoenix.LiveViewTest.live/1`'s `defmacro live(conn, path \\\\ nil, opts \\\\ [])`\n183\t  # embeds a reference to an unqualified `get(conn, path)` call in its `quote`\n184\t  # block for the `is_binary(path)` branch — even calling it as `live(conn)`\n185\t  # (single arg, taking the OTHER branch at runtime) still needs\n186\t  # `Phoenix.ConnTest.get/2` resolvable at COMPILE time for the whole macro\n187\t  # expansion (mirrors logs_live_test.exs's own \"AC12\" describe block comment).\n188\t  import Phoenix.ConnTest\n189\t\n190\t  @endpoint Nebu.Admin.Endpoint\n191\t  @secret_key_base String.duplicate(\"nebu-trace-map-leitstand-test-key\", 2)\n192\t  @topic \"operational_logs\"\n193\t\n194\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n195\t  # Test helpers — mirrors logs_live_test.exs's \"AC12\" describe block's\n196\t  # admin_conn_for_live/1 EXACTLY (see this file's moduledoc for why every\n197\t  # test here must go through the real router rather than live_isolated/3).\n198\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n199\t\n200\t  defp admin_conn(path) do\n201\t    %{conn(:get, path) | secret_key_base: @secret_key_base}\n202\t    |> @endpoint.admin_session([])\n203\t    |> fetch_session()\n204\t    |> put_session(\"current_user\", %{\n205\t      \"admin\" => true,\n206\t      \"sub\" => \"admin-trace-map-leitstand-tests\",\n207\t      \"matrix_user_id\" => \"@admin:test.local\"\n208\t    })\n209\t  end\n210\t\n211\t  defp mount_trace_map(query_string \\\\ \"\") do\n212\t    path = if query_string == \"\", do: \"/admin/logs/trace-map\", else: \"/admin/logs/trace-map?\" <> query_string\n213\t    sent_conn = path |> admin_conn() |> @endpoint.call(@endpoint.init([]))\n214\t    live(sent_conn)\n215\t  end\n216\t\n217\t  defp parsed(html), do: Floki.parse_document!(html)\n218\t\n219\t  defp find(html, selector), do: html |> Floki.find(selector) |> List.first()\n220\t  defp find_all(html, selector), do: Floki.find(html, selector)\n221\t  defp attr(node, name), do: node |> List.wrap() |> Floki.attribute(name) |> List.first()\n222\t\n223\t  defp node_locator(html, key) do\n224\t    find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"#{key}\"]))\n225\t  end\n226\t\n227\t  defp entry(overrides) do\n228\t    Map.merge(\n229\t      %{\n230\t        level: \"info\",\n231\t        source: \"http\",\n232\t        route_template: \"/foo\",\n233\t        request_id: \"req-\" <> Integer.to_string(System.unique_integer([:positive])),\n234\t        message: \"distinctive-trace-map-leitstand-probe\",\n235\t        metadata: %{}\n236\t      },\n237\t      Map.new(overrides)\n238\t    )\n239\t  end\n240\t\n241\t  defp broadcast_entry(overrides) do\n242\t    e = entry(overrides)\n243\t    Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(Nebu.PubSub, @topic, e)\n244\t    e\n245\t  end\n246\t\n247\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n248\t  # AC1: New LiveView route and active-nav repoint\n249\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n250\t\n251\t  describe \"AC1: /admin/logs/trace-map mounts the new LiveView with the correct active-nav\" do\n252\t    test \"mounting succeeds and highlights the Trace Map child nav item, not Live Stream\" do\n253\t      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n254\t      doc = parsed(html)\n255\t\n256\t      trace_map_link = find(doc, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"logs-trace-map\"]))\n257\t      assert attr(trace_map_link, \"aria-current\") == \"page\",\n258\t             \"AC1: the Trace Map nav child must be the active one, not Live Stream\"\n259\t\n260\t      live_stream_link = find(doc, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"logs-monitoring\"]))\n261\t      refute attr(live_stream_link, \"aria-current\") == \"page\"\n262\t    end\n263\t\n264\t    test \"the page title mentions Trace Map\" do\n265\t      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n266\t      assert html =~ \"Trace Map\"\n267\t    end\n268\t  end\n269\t\n270\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n271\t  # AC2: Radial SVG map layout and hierarchy\n272\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n273\t\n274\t  describe \"AC2: radial SVG map — nebu is always present, domains/satellites appear as observed\" do\n275\t    test \"on mount with no traffic yet, only the nebu node is rendered, at the spec's viewBox\" do\n276\t      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n277\t      doc = parsed(html)\n278\t\n279\t      svg = find(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-svg\"]))\n280\t      assert attr(svg, \"viewBox\") == \"-560 -400 1120 800\"\n281\t\n282\t      nebu = node_locator(doc, \"nebu\")\n283\t      assert attr(nebu, \"data-node-kind\") == \"nebu\"\n284\t\n285\t      assert find_all(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-kind=\"domain\"])) == []\n286\t    end\n287\t\n288\t    test \"observing traffic for two different domains renders both domain groups and their satellites\" do\n289\t      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n290\t\n291\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", level: \"info\")\n292\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\")\n293\t\n294\t      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n295\t\n296\t      client_domain = node_locator(doc, \"/_matrix/client\")\n297\t      admin_domain = node_locator(doc, \"/admin\")\n298\t      assert client_domain, \"expected a domain node for /_matrix/client\"\n299\t      assert admin_domain, \"expected a domain node for /admin\"\n300\t      assert attr(client_domain, \"data-node-kind\") == \"domain\"\n301\t\n302\t      sync_satellite = node_locator(doc, \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\")\n303\t      users_satellite = node_locator(doc, \"/admin/users\")\n304\t      assert attr(sync_satellite, \"data-node-kind\") == \"satellite\"\n305\t      assert attr(users_satellite, \"data-node-kind\") == \"satellite\"\n306\t    end\n307\t  end\n308\t\n309\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n310\t  # AC4: Visual states (ok/warn/error/selected) — off/temp deliberately\n311\t  # excluded, see note below.\n312\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n313\t\n314\t  describe \"AC4: visual states — worst-child propagation from satellite to domain to nebu\" do\n315\t    # NOTE on scope: the story's own \"Known Gaps (Out of Scope, Follow-up\n316\t    # Story)\" section explicitly defers \"Long-polling endpoints off by\n317\t    # default\" and \"Temporary capture\" (the \"off\"/\"temp\" states) to the\n318\t    # `trace-map-node-controls` story, even though AC4's states TABLE lists\n319\t    # all 6 states. No endpoint is classified as off-tier BY THIS STORY, so\n320\t    # there is no reachable off/temp scenario to test yet — flagged\n321\t    # explicitly in the ATDD handoff report as a scope tension for the\n322\t    # dev/PM to confirm, rather than guessed at here.\n323\t\n324\t    test \"a warning on a satellite propagates warn status up through its domain to nebu\" do\n325\t      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n326\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", level: \"warning\")\n327\t\n328\t      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n329\t\n330\t      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"warn\"\n331\t      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"/_matrix/client\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"warn\"\n332\t      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"nebu\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"warn\"\n333\t    end\n334\t\n335\t    test \"an error outranks a warning at every ancestor level\" do\n336\t      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n337\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", level: \"warning\")\n338\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/_matrix/client/v3/login\", level: \"error\")\n339\t\n340\t      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n341\t\n342\t      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"warn\"\n343\t      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"/_matrix/client/v3/login\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"error\"\n344\t      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"/_matrix/client\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"error\"\n345\t      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"nebu\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"error\"\n346\t    end\n347\t\n348\t    test \"an unrelated healthy domain does not inherit another domain's error\" do\n349\t      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n350\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", level: \"error\")\n351\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\")\n352\t\n353\t      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n354\t\n355\t      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"/_matrix/client\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"error\"\n356\t      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"/admin\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"ok\"\n357\t      # nebu is the worst of ALL domains, so it still reflects the error.\n358\t      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"nebu\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"error\"\n359\t    end\n360\t\n361\t    test \"warn/error nodes carry a numeric badge count; a healthy node does not\" do\n362\t      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n363\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"warning\")\n364\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"warning\")\n365\t\n366\t      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n367\t      node = node_locator(doc, \"/admin/users\")\n368\t\n369\t      assert find(node, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-badge-warn\"])) |> Floki.text() =~ \"2\"\n370\t      assert find(node, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-badge-error\"])) == nil\n371\t    end\n372\t  end\n373\t\n374\t  describe \"AC4/AC5: selection is orthogonal to health status\" do\n375\t    test \"selecting a healthy node marks it selected without changing its health status\" do\n376\t      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n377\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\")\n378\t      render(view)\n379\t\n380\t      html =\n381\t        view\n382\t        |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"/admin/users\"]))\n383\t        |> render_click()\n384\t        |> parsed()\n385\t\n386\t      node = node_locator(html, \"/admin/users\")\n387\t      assert attr(node, \"data-node-status\") == \"ok\"\n388\t      assert attr(node, \"data-node-selected\") == \"true\"\n389\t      assert attr(node, \"aria-pressed\") == \"true\"\n390\t    end\n391\t  end\n392\t\n393\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n394\t  # AC5: Interactive filtering — click/Enter/Space toggle, multi-select OR,\n395\t  # domain convenience, clear all, chips, URL query-param round trip\n396\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n397\t\n398\t  describe \"AC5: clicking a satellite toggles it as a stream filter and updates the URL\" do\n399\t    test \"clicking once selects the node, adds a chip, and patches the URL\" do\n400\t      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n401\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\")\n402\t      render(view)\n403\t\n404\t      html =\n405\t        view\n406\t        |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"/admin/users\"]))\n407\t        |> render_click()\n408\t        |> parsed()\n409\t\n410\t      assert attr(node_locator(html, \"/admin/users\"), \"aria-pressed\") == \"true\"\n411\t      chip = find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip\"][data-chip-value=\"/admin/users\"]))\n412\t      assert chip, \"AC5: expected a filter chip for the newly-selected node\"\n413\t\n414\t      assert_patch(view, \"/admin/logs/trace-map?endpoint%5B%5D=%2Fadmin%2Fusers\")\n415\t    end\n416\t\n417\t    test \"clicking the same node again deselects it and removes its chip\" do\n418\t      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n419\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\")\n420\t      render(view)\n421\t\n422\t      view\n423\t      |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"/admin/users\"]))\n424\t      |> render_click()\n425\t\n426\t      html =\n427\t        view\n428\t        |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"/admin/users\"]))\n429\t        |> render_click()\n430\t        |> parsed()\n431\t\n432\t      assert attr(node_locator(html, \"/admin/users\"), \"aria-pressed\") == \"false\"\n433\t      refute find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip\"][data-chip-value=\"/admin/users\"]))\n434\t    end\n435\t  end\n436\t\n437\t  describe \"AC5: multi-select is OR-within-the-endpoint-dimension\" do\n438\t    test \"selecting two different satellites shows two chips, both aria-pressed=true\" describe \"AC6: war-room banner — hidden by default (no active session, no Nebu.Repo)\" do\n569\t    test \"the banner element exists but is hidden, and carries the live-region contract\" do\n570\t      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n571\t      banner = find(parsed(html), ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-banner\"]))\n572\t\n573\t      assert banner, \"AC6: the banner must always be present in markup (toggled via `hidden`)\"\n574\t      assert attr(banner, \"hidden\") == \"\",\n575\t             \"AC6: with no active session, the banner must carry the boolean `hidden` attribute\"\n576\t      assert attr(banner, \"role\") == \"status\"\n577\t      assert attr(banner, \"aria-live\") == \"polite\"\n578\t    end\n579\t  end\n580\t\n581\t  describe \"AC6: war-room banner — active session (requires a real session + Nebu.Repo)\" do\n582\t    @describetag :integration\n583\t\n584\t    setup do\n585\t      Nebu.Release.migrate()\n586\t      :ok\n587\t    end\n588\t\n589\t    test \"an active war-room session renders the banner with recording dot, user, and an Open War Room link\" do\n590\t      {:ok, session} =\n591\t        Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session(%{\n592\t          started_by: \"@alice:nebu.example\",\n593\t          reason: \"trace-map-leitstand banner ATDD probe\",\n594\t          max_entries: 100,\n595\t          max_duration_s: 300\n596\t        })\n597\t\n598\t      on_exit(fn -> Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.stop_session(session.id) end)\n599\t\n600\t      {:ok, _view, html} = session must NOT carry `hidden`\"\n605\t      assert find(banner, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-dot\"]))\n606\t      assert Floki.text(banner) =~ \"@alice:nebu.example\"\n607\t\n608\t ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n614\t  # AC7: Tooltip content (data attributes only — show/hide is E2E-only)\n615\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n616\t\n617\t  describe \"AC7: every node carries the data a hover/focus tooltip needs, with no sensitive content\" do\n618\t =~ ~r/filter/i\n629\t\n630\t      refute attr(node, \"data-tooltip-counts\") =~ \"distinctive-trace-map-leitstand-probe\",\n631\t             \"AC7/spec §8: no log message content may leak into \"aria-pressed\") == \"true\"\n724\t      end\n725\t    end\n726\t  end\n727\t\n728\t  describe \"AC9: leitstand shortcut — click one while all active isolates it; click it again restores all\" pill = find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"#{level}\"]))\n743\t        assert attr(pill, \"aria-pressed\") == \"false\"\n744\t      end\n745\t    end\n746\t\n747\t    test \"clicking the sole active level again restores all 4 to active\" do\n748\t      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n749\t\n750\t      view\n751\t      |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"INFO\"]))\n752\t      |> render_click()\n753\t\n754\t      html =\n755\t        view\n756\t        |> \"ERROR\"] do\n761\t        pill = find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"#{level}\"]))\n762\t        assert attr(pill, \"aria-pressed\") == \"true\"\n763\t      end\n764\t    end\n765\t\n766\t    test \"clicking a level when SEVERAL (not all) are active is a plain toggle, not the shortcut\" do\n767\t      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n768\t\n769\t      # Isolate INFO (all -> one), then toggle WARN back on: now {INFO, WARN}\n770\t      # are active, i.e. \"several but not all\" — the next click must be a\n771\t      # plain toggle, not re-trigger the shortcut.\n772\t      view |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"INFO\"])) |> render_click()\n773\t\n774\t      view\n775\t      |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"WARN\"]))\n776\t      |> render_click()\n777\t\n778\t      html =\n779\t        view\n780\t        |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"ERROR\"]))\n781\t        |> render_click()\n782\t        |> parsed()\n783\t\n784\t      for level <- [\"INFO\", \"WARN\", \"ERROR\"] do\n785\t        pill = find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"#{level}\"]))\n786\t        assert attr(pill, \"aria-pressed\") == \"true\", \"expected #{level} active\"\n787\t      end\n788\t\n789\t      debug_pill = find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"DEBUG\"]))\n790\t      assert attr(debug_pill, \"aria-pressed\") == \"false\"\n791\t    end\n792\t  end\n793\t\n794\t  describe \"AC9: stream content is filtered by level AND by the active endpoint selection\" do\n795\t    test \"deactivating WARN hides warning-level lines but keeps info-level lines\" do\n796\t      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n797\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\", message: \"distinctive-info-line\")\n798\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"warning\", message: \"distinctive-warn-line\")\n799\t      render(view)\n800\t\n801\t      html =\n802\t        view\n803\t        |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"WARN\"]))\n804\t        |> render_click()\n805\t        |> parsed()\n806\t\n807\t      lines = find_all(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-stream-line\"]))\n808\t      texts = Enum.map(lines, &Floki.text/1)\n809\t\n810\t      assert Enum.any?(texts, &(&1 =~ \"distinctive-info-line\"))\n811\t      refute Enum.any?(texts, &(&1 =~ \"distinctive-warn-line\"))\n812\t    end\n813\t\n814\t    test \"selecting a node's filter narrows the stream to that endpoint's lines\" do\n815\t      {:ok, view, \"/admin/rooms\", level: \"info\", message: \"distinctive-rooms-line\")\n818\t      render(view)\n819\t\n820\t      html =\n821\t        view\n822\t        |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"/admin/users\"]))\n823\t        |> render_click()\n824\t        |> parsed()\n825\t\n826\t      texts = html |> find_all(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-stream-line\"])) |> Enum.map(&Floki.text/1)\n827\t\n828\t      assert Enum.any?(texts, &(&1 =~ \"distinctive-users-line\"))\n829\t      refute Enum.any?(texts, &(&1 =~ \"distinctive-rooms-line\"))\n830\t    end\n831\t  end\n832\t\n833\t  describe \"AC9: the resizer's static, accessible markup contract\" do\n834\t    test \"role=separator, aria-orientation=horizontal, tabindex=0, has a label\" do\n835\t      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n836\t      resizer = find(parsed(html), ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-resizer\"]))\n837\t\n838\t      assert attr(resizer, \"role\") == \"separator\"\n839\t      assert attr(resizer, \"aria-orientation\") == \"horizontal\"\n840\t      assert ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n846\t  # AC10: Accessibility — blocking requirements\n847\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n848\t\n849\t  describe \"AC10: every node is a focusable, labeled, toggle-state-exposing element\" do\n850\t    test \"a satellite node's aria-label mentions its route, status, and counts\" do\n851\t      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n852\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"error\")\n853\t\n854\t      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n855\t      node = node_locator(doc, \"/admin/users\")\n856\t\n857\t      assert attr(node, \"role\") == \"button\"\n858\t      assert attr(node, \"tabindex\") == \"0\"\n859\t\n860\t      label = attr(node, \"aria-label\")\n861\t      assert label =~ \"/admin/users\"\n862\t      assert label =~ ~r/error/i\n863\t      assert label =~ ~r/\\d+ (error|warning|request)/\n864\t    end\n865\t\n866\t    test \"nebu's aria-label mentions \\\"all traffic\\\" and its overall status\" do\n867\t      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n868\t      nebu = node_locator(parsed(html), \"nebu\")\n869\t\n870\t      label = attr(nebu, \"aria-label\")\n871\t      assert label =~ \"nebu\"\n872\t      assert label =~ ~r/healthy|ok/i\n873\t    end\n874\t  end\n875\t\n876\t  describe \"AC10: a live region announces newly-detected errors\" do\n877\t    test \"the announcer region is present with naming the endpoint and count, with no message content\" do\n887\t      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n888\t      broadcast_entry(\n889\t        route_template: \"/admin/users\",\n890\t ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-announcer\"]))\n896\t      assert announcer, \"expected the announcer region to be present\"\n897\t\n898\t      text = Floki.text(announcer)\n899\t\n900\t      assert text
