their satellites (natural Tab order)\" do\n906\t    test \"nebu -> domain -> its satellites, in document order\" do\n907\t      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n908\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\")\n909\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/rooms\", level: \"info\")\n910\t\n911\t      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n912\t      keys = doc |> find_all(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"])) |> Enum.map(&attr(&1, \"data-node-key\"))\n913\t\n914\t      assert Enum.find_index(keys, &(&1 == \"nebu\")) == 0\n915\t      admin_index = Enum.find_index(keys, &(&1 == \"/admin\"))\n916\t      users_index = Enum.find_index(keys, &(&1 == \"/admin/users\"))\n917\t      rooms_index = Enum.find_index(keys, &(&1 == \"/admin/rooms\"))\n918\t\n919\t      assert admin_index < users_index\n920\t      assert admin_index < rooms_index\n921\t    end\n922\t  end\n923\t\n924\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n925\t  # AC11: CSP-compliant client-side behavior (static markup proxy —\n926\t  # real hook wiring is exercised by app.js + logs-trace-map.feature)\n927\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n928\t\n929\t  describe \"AC11: no inline <script> tags or inline event-handler attributes anywhere on the page\" do\n930\t    test \"the rendered page contains no INLINE <script> tag (external, src=-bearing tags are fine) and no on*= inline handlers\" do\n931\t      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n932\t\n933\t      # The root layout (Nebu.Admin.Layouts.root/1) legitimately renders several\n934\t      # EXTERNAL `<script src=\"...\">` tags (phoenix.min.js, app.js, ...) — CSP\n935\t      # `script-src 'self'` allows those; AC11 forbids INLINE script content\n936\t      # only (no `src` attribute) — a `<script ...>` tag NOT immediately\n937\t      # followed by a `src=` attribute anywhere before its closing `>`.\n938\t      refute Regex.match?(~r/<script(?![^>]*\\bsrc=)[^>]*>/i, html),\n939\t             \"AC11 (script-src 'self'): no INLINE <script> tag (without a src=...) \" <>\n940\t               \"may appear in server-rendered HTML\"\n941\t\n942\t      refute Regex.match?(~r/\\son[a-z]+\\s*=/i, html),\n943\t             \"AC11: no inline onclick=/onmouseover=/etc. event-handler attribute may appear\"\n944\t    end\n945\t  end\n946\t\n947\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n948\t  # AC14: UX Gate Advisory A1 — Logs \"Configuration\" child renamed\n949\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n950\t\n951\t  describe \"AC14: the Logs submenu's submenu to still contain a logs-config child\"\n958\t\n959\t      label = Floki.text(logs_config_link) |> String.trim()\n960\t\n961\t      refute label == \"Configuration\",\n962\t             \"AC14 Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapTest do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  RED-phase ATDD tests for `enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand`\n  (AC1, AC2, AC4, AC5, AC6, AC7, AC8, AC9, AC10, AC11, AC14 — the LiveView\n  integration half; geometry and status-model pure-module contracts live in\n  `trace_map_geometry_test.exs` / `trace_map_status_test.exs`; the old\n  embedded-Trace-Map retirement, AC13, lives in `trace_map_retirement_test.exs`;\n  the browser-only half of every AC below — real keyboard focus, pointer\n  drag, hover tooltips, `prefers-reduced-motion` — lives in\n  `e2e/features/admin/logs-trace-map.feature`).\n\n  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand.md\n  Design: _bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-spec.md\n\n  ## Why these MUST FAIL right now\n\n  Neither `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap` nor the `/admin/logs/trace-map`\n  route (currently a stub pointing at the OLD `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive, :index`\n  — see `router.ex`) exist yet in the shape this file assumes. Every test\n  below fails at `live/1` with either a 404/redirect (stub route still\n  serves the old page) or `UndefinedFunctionError` once the route is\n  updated but the module is still missing — NOT a compile error in this\n  file.\n\n  ## Architecture decision this file pins down: a SEPARATE LiveView module,\n  not a new action on the existing `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`\n\n  AC1's literal text says \"the LiveView module `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive` (or\n  alias) is extended with a new `mount/3` action for trace-map\". This file\n  deliberately pins the \"(or alias)\" branch — a NEW, SEPARATE module\n  `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap` — for a concrete, load-bearing reason\n  already documented in the EXISTING `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s own moduledoc\n  (see `logs_live.ex`, \"Trace Map filter toggle is server-tracked\" section):\n  merely EXPORTING a `handle_params/3` callback on a LiveView module breaks\n  EVERY `live_isolated/3`-mounted test in that module's own suite (empirically\n  verified against the real, installed `phoenix_live_view` dependency —\n  `live_isolated/3` unconditionally rejects `handle_params/3` regardless of\n  whether a patch is ever attempted). AC5 HARD-REQUIRES `handle_params/3`\n  for this new page (the whole point of the story, per the predecessor\n  `trace-map-filter-reset-bugfix`'s lesson: URL-driven state, not\n  client-DOM-only). Adding `handle_params/3` to the EXISTING, shared\n  `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive` module would therefore break all ~200 pre-existing\n  `live_isolated/3`-based tests in `logs_live_test.exs` for the UNRELATED\n  `:index` (Live Stream) action — a story-scope-violating regression this\n  file's own architecture avoids entirely by giving the Leitstand page its\n  own module. The dev agent may still choose a different module name/split\n  IF the rendered markup and the router contract below hold; the LOAD-BEARING\n  constraint is \"not the same module as the existing `:index` action\".\n\n  Router contract (replaces the existing stub in `router.ex`):\n\n      live \"/logs/trace-map\", LogsLive.TraceMap, :index\n\n  `@active_nav` is set to `\"logs-trace-map\"` in `mount/3`.\n\n  ## Why (almost) every test below is router-mounted, not `live_isolated/3`\n\n  Per the architecture decision above, `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap` DOES\n  define `handle_params/3` — so `live_isolated/3` cannot mount it at all\n  (see above). Every test therefore dispatches through the REAL router via\n  `Phoenix.LiveViewTest.live/1` + a real, SENT conn (mirrors\n  `logs_live_test.exs`'s own \"AC12: mount/3's war_room_id param\" describe\n  block's `admin_conn_for_live/1` helper EXACTLY). Despite being\n  router-mounted, MOST tests below still run under plain `mix test`\n  (`make test-unit-elixir`, no `Nebu.Repo`) because:\n\n    * `Nebu.Admin.Auth.bootstrap_completed?/0` defaults to `true` when\n      neither the `:admin_bootstrap_store` seam nor `Nebu.Repo` is running\n      (see `auth.ex`'s own doc comment) — the `on_mount(:require_admin)`\n      gate does not redirect these tests to `/admin/bootstrap`.\n    * `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`'s `mount/3` MUST guard every DB read\n      (initial endpoint/domain history, war-room status) behind\n      `Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo)`, mirroring the EXISTING `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s\n      own `initial_entries/1`/`initial_war_room_status/0` precedent — this\n      is a REQUIRED contract, not optional, precisely so this file's tests\n      run DB-free.\n    * `Nebu.PubSub` (unlike `Nebu.Repo`) IS running in the plain unit-test\n      environment (confirmed by the existing `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s own\n      `mount/3` unconditionally subscribing to it, exercised by hundreds of\n      already-passing `live_isolated/3` tests) — so tests can drive live\n      status updates via a real `Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast/3` on the SAME\n      `\"operational_logs\"` topic the existing page already uses, with no\n      DB involved.\n\n  The ONE describe block that genuinely needs a live session/DB (the\n  war-room banner's ACTIVE state) is tagged `@describetag :integration` and\n  mirrors the existing `admin_conn_for_live/1` + `Nebu.Release.migrate/0` +\n  raw-SQL-seed pattern from `logs_live_test.exs`'s AC12 block — it will not\n  run under `make test-unit-elixir`, only `make test-integration`, exactly\n  like that existing precedent.\n\n  ## Correction: AC5/AC12's literal repeated bare `?endpoint=X&endpoint=Y`\n  URL format does not survive `Plug.Conn.Query.decode/1`\n\n  AC5/AC12 Scenario 10 both show a bookmarked URL as\n  `?endpoint=%2Fsync&endpoint=%2Fsend` — repeated BARE (non-bracket) query\n  keys. `Plug.Conn.Query.decode/1` documents \"if a value is given more than\n  once, the LAST value takes precedence\" for a bare repeated key (the\n  EXACT same footgun `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s own `active_filters_from_params/2`\n  comment already flags, which is why that sibling page comma-joins a\n  single value instead). This story's AC5, unlike the sibling page, does\n  NOT propose comma-joining — so this file instead pins the\n  BRACKET-ARRAY query format `?endpoint[]=X&endpoint[]=Y`, which\n  `Plug.Conn.Query.decode/1` natively decodes into `%{\"endpoint\" => [\"X\",\n  \"Y\"]}` with no data loss and no custom parsing. This is a genuine\n  discrepancy between the story's literal example and Plug's real decoding\n  behavior — flagged here (and in the ATDD handoff report) for the dev/PM\n  to explicitly confirm; the dev agent may instead hand-parse\n  `conn.query_string` directly to honor the story's literal bare-repeated\n  format IF the rendered `handle_params/3` behavior below (multi-select\n  round-trips correctly) still holds.\n\n  ## Data-attribute / event contract this file pins down\n\n    * SVG root: `[data-testid=\"trace-map-svg\"]`, `viewBox=\"-560 -400 1120 800\"`.\n    * Every node (nebu/domain/satellite): `[data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"]\n      [data-node-key=...] [data-node-kind=\"nebu\"|\"domain\"|\"satellite\"]\n      [data-node-status=\"ok\"|\"warn\"|\"error\"] [data-node-selected=\"true\"|\"false\"]\n      role=\"button\" tabindex=\"0\" aria-pressed=\"true\"|\"false\" aria-label=\"...\".`\n      Nebu's own `aria-pressed` is always `\"false\"` (it is not itself a\n      toggle target — clicking it CLEARS filters instead, spec §2/AC5).\n    * Click contract: non-nebu nodes carry `phx-click=\"toggle_node\"\n      phx-value-key={key}`; nebu carries `phx-click=\"clear_filters\"`. Unlike\n      the sibling `admin-logs-monitoring-view` page's Trace Map bubbles,\n      this is an ORDINARY server round-trip (no AC3-style\n      client-side-only/zero-lag constraint applies to this story) — a plain\n      `phx-click` event name, NOT a `Phoenix.LiveView.JS` command list.\n    * Filter chips: `[data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip\"]\n      [data-chip-value=...]`, each containing\n      `[data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip-remove\"]` (same `toggle_node`\n      handler, removing = toggling off); `[data-testid=\"trace-map-clear-all\"]`\n      (`phx-click=\"clear_filters\"`).\n    * War-room banner: `[data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-banner\"]`, `hidden`\n      attribute present when inactive, `role=\"status\" aria-live=\"polite\"`;\n      recording dot `[data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-dot\"]`; open-link\n      `[data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-open-link\"]` navigating to\n      `/admin/logs/war-room`.\n    * Tooltip data (AC7 — content only; show/hide on hover/focus is\n      app.js/E2E territory): every node ALSO carries `data-tooltip-name`,\n      `data-tooltip-route`, `data-tooltip-status`, `data-tooltip-counts`,\n      `data-tooltip-cta` attributes with the values a hover/focus JS hook\n      would render into the shared `[data-testid=\"trace-map-tooltip\"]`\n      element — no payload/token/message content, per spec §8's\n      no-sensitive-data constraint.\n    * Inspector: `[data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector\"]`, empty state\n      `[data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-empty\"]`; once a node has been\n      clicked/inspected: `[data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-name\"]`,\n      `-route`, `-status-pill][data-status=...]`, `-stat-requests`,\n      `-stat-warnings`, `-stat-errors`, and one\n      `[data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-event\"]` per recent event (newest\n      first, capped at 5).\n    * Legend: `[data-testid=\"trace-map-legend\"]` containing exactly 6\n      `[data-testid=\"trace-map-legend-row\"][data-legend-state=...]` rows\n      (`ok`, `warn`, `error`, `selected`, `off`, `temp`) — ALWAYS rendered,\n      regardless of what is currently on the map (spec §2's \"always\n      visible\" legend).\n    * Stream panel: `[data-testid=\"trace-map-stream\"]`; log lines\n      `[data-testid=\"trace-map-stream-line\"][data-level=...][data-route=...]`;\n      level pills `[data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"DEBUG\"|\n      \"INFO\"|\"WARN\"|\"ERROR\"]` `aria-pressed`, `phx-click=\"toggle_level\"\n      phx-value-level={level}` — internally mapped to the lowercase\n      `Nebu.Web.LogStore` level strings via `%{\"DEBUG\" => \"debug\", \"INFO\" =>\n      \"info\", \"WARN\" => \"warning\", \"ERROR\" => \"error\"}` (the pill vocabulary\n      is the spec's own abbreviated `WARN`/`ERROR`; `LogStore` entries use\n      `\"warning\"`/`\"error\"` — this mapping is the dev agent's own\n      responsibility to get right, pinned here explicitly since it is an\n      easy silent-mismatch trap).\n    * Resizer: `[data-testid=\"trace-map-resizer\"] role=\"separator\"\n      aria-orientation=\"horizontal\" tabindex=\"0\" aria-label=...` — actual\n      pointer-drag/keyboard resize behavior is CSS/JS-driven and only\n      testable in a real browser (`logs-trace-map.feature` Scenario 9);\n      this file only pins the static, accessible markup contract.\n    * Live announcer: `[data-testid=\"trace-map-announcer\"] role=\"status\"\n      aria-live=\"polite\"`.\n  \"\"\"\n\n  use ExUnit.Case, async: false\n\n  import Plug.Test\n  import Plug.Conn\n  import Phoenix.LiveViewTest\n  # `Phoenix.LiveViewTest.live/1`'s `defmacro live(conn, path \\\\ nil, opts \\\\ [])`\n  # embeds a reference to an unqualified `get(conn, path)` call in its `quote`\n  # block for the `is_binary(path)` branch — even calling it as `live(conn)`\n  # (single arg, taking the OTHER branch at runtime) still needs\n  # `Phoenix.ConnTest.get/2` resolvable at COMPILE time for the whole macro\n  # expansion (mirrors logs_live_test.exs's own \"AC12\" describe block comment).\n  import Phoenix.ConnTest\n\n  @endpoint Nebu.Admin.Endpoint\n  @secret_key_base String.duplicate(\"nebu-trace-map-leitstand-test-key\", 2)\n  @topic \"operational_logs\"\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Test helpers — mirrors logs_live_test.exs's \"AC12\" describe block's\n  # admin_conn_for_live/1 EXACTLY (see this file's moduledoc for why every\n  # test here must go through the real router rather than live_isolated/3).\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  defp admin_conn(path) do\n    %{conn(:get, path) | secret_key_base: @secret_key_base}\n    |> @endpoint.admin_session([])\n    |> fetch_session()\n    |> put_session(\"current_user\", %{\n      \"admin\" => true,\n      \"sub\" => \"admin-trace-map-leitstand-tests\",\n      \"matrix_user_id\" => \"@admin:test.local\"\n    })\n  end\n\n  defp mount_trace_map(query_string \\\\ \"\") do\n    path = if query_string == \"\", do: \"/admin/logs/trace-map\", else: \"/admin/logs/trace-map?\" <> query_string\n    sent_conn = path |> admin_conn() |> @endpoint.call(@endpoint.init([]))\n    live(sent_conn)\n  end\n\n  defp parsed(html), do: Floki.parse_document!(html)\n\n  defp find(html, selector), do: html |> Floki.find(selector) |> List.first()\n  defp find_all(html, selector), do: Floki.find(html, selector)\n  defp attr(node, name), do: node |> List.wrap() |> Floki.attribute(name) |> List.first()\n\n  defp node_locator(html, key) do\n    find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"#{key}\"]))\n  end\n\n  defp entry(overrides) do\n    Map.merge(\n      %{\n        level: \"info\",\n        source: \"http\",\n        route_template: \"/foo\",\n        request_id: \"req-\" <> Integer.to_string(System.unique_integer([:positive])),\n        message: \"distinctive-trace-map-leitstand-probe\",\n        metadata: %{}\n      },\n      Map.new(overrides)\n    )\n  end\n\n  defp broadcast_entry(overrides) do\n    e = entry(overrides)\n    Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(Nebu.PubSub, @topic, e)\n    e\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # AC1: New LiveView route and active-nav repoint\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"AC1: /admin/logs/trace-map mounts the new LiveView with the correct active-nav\" do\n    test \"mounting succeeds and highlights the Trace Map child nav item, not Live Stream\" do\n      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n      doc = parsed(html)\n\n      trace_map_link = find(doc, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"logs-trace-map\"]))\n      assert attr(trace_map_link, \"aria-current\") == \"page\",\n             \"AC1: the Trace Map nav child must be the active one, not Live Stream\"\n\n      live_stream_link = find(doc, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"logs-monitoring\"]))\n      refute attr(live_stream_link, \"aria-current\") == \"page\"\n    end\n\n    test \"the page title mentions Trace Map\" do\n      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n      assert html =~ \"Trace Map\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # AC2: Radial SVG map layout and hierarchy\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"AC2: radial SVG map — nebu is always present, domains/satellites appear as observed\" do\n    test \"on mount with no traffic yet, only the nebu node is rendered, at the spec's viewBox\" do\n      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n      doc = parsed(html)\n\n      svg = find(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-svg\"]))\n      assert attr(svg, \"viewBox\") == \"-560 -400 1120 800\"\n\n      nebu = node_locator(doc, \"nebu\")\n      assert attr(nebu, \"data-node-kind\") == \"nebu\"\n\n      assert find_all(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-kind=\"domain\"])) == []\n    end\n\n    test \"observing traffic for two different domains renders both domain groups and their satellites\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", level: \"info\")\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\")\n\n      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n\n      client_domain = node_locator(doc, \"/_matrix/client\")\n      admin_domain = node_locator(doc, \"/admin\")\n      assert client_domain, \"expected a domain node for /_matrix/client\"\n      assert admin_domain, \"expected a domain node for /admin\"\n      assert attr(client_domain, \"data-node-kind\") == \"domain\"\n\n      sync_satellite = node_locator(doc, \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\")\n      users_satellite = node_locator(doc, \"/admin/users\")\n      assert attr(sync_satellite, \"data-node-kind\") == \"satellite\"\n      assert attr(users_satellite, \"data-node-kind\") == \"satellite\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # AC4: Visual states (ok/warn/error/selected) — off/temp deliberately\n  # excluded, see note below.\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"AC4: visual states — worst-child propagation from satellite to domain to nebu\" do\n    # NOTE on scope: the story's own \"Known Gaps (Out of Scope, Follow-up\n    # Story)\" section explicitly defers \"Long-polling endpoints off by\n    # default\" and \"Temporary capture\" (the \"off\"/\"temp\" states) to the\n    # `trace-map-node-controls` story, even though AC4's states TABLE lists\n    # all 6 states. No endpoint is classified as off-tier BY THIS STORY, so\n    # there is no reachable off/temp scenario to test yet — flagged\n    # explicitly in the ATDD handoff report as a scope tension for the\n    # dev/PM to confirm, rather than guessed at here.\n\n    test \"a warning on a satellite propagates warn status up through its domain to nebu\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", level: \"warning\")\n\n      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n\n      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"warn\"\n      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"/_matrix/client\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"warn\"\n      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"nebu\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"warn\"\n    end\n\n    test \"an error outranks a warning at every ancestor level\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", level: \"warning\")\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/_matrix/client/v3/login\", level: \"error\")\n\n      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n\n      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"warn\"\n      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"/_matrix/client/v3/login\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"error\"\n      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"/_matrix/client\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"error\"\n      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"nebu\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"error\"\n    end\n\n    test \"an unrelated healthy domain does not inherit another domain's error\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", level: \"error\")\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\")\n\n      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n\n      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"/_matrix/client\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"error\"\n      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"/admin\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"ok\"\n      # nebu is the worst of ALL domains, so it still reflects the error.\n      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"nebu\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"error\"\n    end\n\n    test \"warn/error nodes carry a numeric badge count; a healthy node does not\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"warning\")\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"warning\")\n\n      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n      node = node_locator(doc, \"/admin/users\")\n\n      assert find(node, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-badge-warn\"])) |> Floki.text() =~ \"2\"\n      assert find(node, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-badge-error\"])) == nil\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC4/AC5: selection is orthogonal to health status\" do\n    test \"selecting a healthy node marks it selected without changing its health status\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\")\n      render(view)\n\n      html =\n        view\n        |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"/admin/users\"]))\n        |> render_click()\n        |> parsed()\n\n      node = node_locator(html, \"/admin/users\")\n      assert attr(node, \"data-node-status\") == \"ok\"\n      assert attr(node, \"data-node-selected\") == \"true\"\n      assert attr(node, \"aria-pressed\") == \"true\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # AC5: Interactive filtering — click/Enter/Space toggle, multi-select OR,\n  # domain convenience, clear all, chips, URL query-param round trip\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"AC5: clicking a satellite toggles it as a stream filter and updates the URL\" do\n    test \"clicking once selects the node, adds a chip, and patches the URL\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\")\n      render(view)\n\n      html =\n        view\n        |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"/admin/users\"]))\n        |> render_click()\n        |> parsed()\n\n      assert attr(node_locator(html, \"/admin/users\"), \"aria-pressed\") == \"true\"\n      chip = find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip\"][data-chip-value=\"/admin/users\"]))\n      assert chip, \"AC5: expected a filter chip for the newly-selected node\"\n\n      assert_patch(view, \"/admin/logs/trace-map?endpoint%5B%5D=%2Fadmin%2Fusers\")\n    end\n\n    test \"clicking the same node again deselects it and removes its chip\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\")\n      render(view)\n\n      view\n      |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"/admin/users\"]))\n      |> render_click()\n\n      html =\n        view\n        |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"/admin/users\"]))\n        |> render_click()\n        |> parsed()\n\n      assert attr(node_locator(html, \"/admin/users\"), \"aria-pressed\") == \"false\"\n      refute find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip\"][data-chip-value=\"/admin/users\"]))\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC5: multi-select is OR-within-the-endpoint-dimension\" do\n    test \"selecting two different satellites shows two chips, both aria-pressed=true\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\")\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/rooms\", level: \"info\")\n      render(view)\n\n      view\n      |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"/admin/users\"]))\n      |> render_click()\n\n      html =\n        view\n        |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"/admin/rooms\"]))\n        |> render_click()\n        |> parsed()\n\n      assert attr(node_locator(html, \"/admin/users\"), \"aria-pressed\") == \"true\"\n      assert attr(node_locator(html, \"/admin/rooms\"), \"aria-pressed\") == \"true\"\n      assert length(find_all(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip\"]))) == 2\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC5: clicking nebu clears every active filter\" do\n    test \"clear all via the nebu node removes all selections and chips\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\")\n      render(view)\n\n      view\n      |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"/admin/users\"]))\n      |> render_click()\n\n      html =\n        view\n        |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"nebu\"]))\n        |> render_click()\n        |> parsed()\n\n      assert attr(node_locator(html, \"/admin/users\"), \"aria-pressed\") == \"false\"\n      assert find_all(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip\"])) == []\n      assert_patch(view, \"/admin/logs/trace-map\")\n    end\n\n    test \"the stream header's Clear all button does the same thing\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\")\n      render(view)\n\n      view\n      |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"/admin/users\"]))\n      |> render_click()\n\n      html =\n        view\n        |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-clear-all\"]))\n        |> render_click()\n        |> parsed()\n\n      assert find_all(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip\"])) == []\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC5: a filter chip's remove control removes just that one filter\" do\n    test \"removing one of two chips leaves the other active\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\")\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/rooms\", level: \"info\")\n      render(view)\n\n      view\n      |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"/admin/users\"]))\n      |> render_click()\n\n      view\n      |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"/admin/rooms\"]))\n      |> render_click()\n\n      html =\n        view\n        |> element(\n          ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip\"][data-chip-value=\"/admin/users\"] ) <>\n            ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip-remove\"])\n        )\n        |> render_click()\n        |> parsed()\n\n      refute find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip\"][data-chip-value=\"/admin/users\"]))\n      assert find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip\"][data-chip-value=\"/admin/rooms\"]))\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC5: bookmarkable URLs — handle_params/3 seeds @active_filters from the query string\" do\n    test \"a bracket-array ?endpoint[]=...&endpoint[]=... URL pre-selects both nodes on mount\" do\n      {:ok, _view, html} =\n        mount_trace_map(\"endpoint%5B%5D=%2Fadmin%2Fusers&endpoint%5B%5D=%2Fadmin%2Frooms\")\n\n      doc = parsed(html)\n\n      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"/admin/users\"), \"aria-pressed\") == \"true\"\n      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"/admin/rooms\"), \"aria-pressed\") == \"true\"\n      assert length(find_all(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip\"]))) == 2\n    end\n\n    test \"an empty query string selects nothing (baseline, no crash)\" do\n      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n      assert find_all(parsed(html), ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip\"])) == []\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC5: domain convenience — clicking a domain filters to that domain\" do\n    test \"clicking a domain node selects it and its chip appears using the DOMAIN key\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\")\n      render(view)\n\n      html =\n        view\n        |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"/admin\"]))\n        |> render_click()\n        |> parsed()\n\n      assert attr(node_locator(html, \"/admin\"), \"aria-pressed\") == \"true\"\n      assert find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip\"][data-chip-value=\"/admin\"]))\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # AC6: War-room banner\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"AC6: war-room banner — hidden by default (no active session, no Nebu.Repo)\" do\n    test \"the banner element exists but is hidden, and carries the live-region contract\" do\n      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n      banner = find(parsed(html), ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-banner\"]))\n\n      assert banner, \"AC6: the banner must always be present in markup (toggled via `hidden`)\"\n      assert attr(banner, \"hidden\") == \"\",\n             \"AC6: with no active session, the banner must carry the boolean `hidden` attribute\"\n      assert attr(banner, \"role\") == \"status\"\n      assert attr(banner, \"aria-live\") == \"polite\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC6: war-room banner — active session (requires a real session + Nebu.Repo)\" do\n    @describetag :integration\n\n    setup do\n      Nebu.Release.migrate()\n      :ok\n    end\n\n    test \"an active war-room session renders the banner with recording dot, user, and an Open War Room link\" do\n      {:ok, session} =\n        Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session(%{\n          started_by: \"@alice:nebu.example\",\n          reason: \"trace-map-leitstand banner ATDD probe\",\n          max_entries: 100,\n          max_duration_s: 300\n        })\n\n      on_exit(fn -> Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.stop_session(session.id) end)\n\n      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n      doc = parsed(html)\n      banner = find(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-banner\"]))\n\n      refute attr(banner, \"hidden\"), \"AC6: an active session must NOT carry `hidden`\"\n      assert find(banner, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-dot\"]))\n      assert Floki.text(banner) =~ \"@alice:nebu.example\"\n\n      open_link = find(banner, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-open-link\"]))\n      assert attr(open_link, \"href\") == \"/admin/logs/war-room\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # AC7: Tooltip content (data attributes only — show/hide is E2E-only)\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"AC7: every node carries the data a hover/focus tooltip needs, with no sensitive content\" do\n    test \"a satellite node's tooltip data includes name, route, status, and counts\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"warning\")\n\n      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n      node = node_locator(doc, \"/admin/users\")\n\n      assert attr(node, \"data-tooltip-route\") == \"/admin/users\"\n      assert attr(node, \"data-tooltip-status\") =~ ~r/warn/i\n      assert attr(node, \"data-tooltip-counts\") =~ \"1\"\n      assert attr(node, \"data-tooltip-cta\") =~ ~r/filter/i\n\n      refute attr(node, \"data-tooltip-counts\") =~ \"distinctive-trace-map-leitstand-probe\",\n             \"AC7/spec §8: no log message content may leak into tooltip data\"\n    end\n\n    test \"nebu's tooltip route reads \\\"all traffic\\\", per spec §2\" do\n      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n      nebu = node_locator(parsed(html), \"nebu\")\n      assert attr(nebu, \"data-tooltip-route\") == \"all traffic\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # AC8: Inspector panel + Legend\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"AC8: inspector panel — empty state, then populated once a node is clicked\" do\n    test \"on mount, the inspector shows its empty-state copy\" do\n      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n      doc = parsed(html)\n\n      assert find(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-empty\"])),\n             \"AC8: expected the empty-state hint before any node is selected\"\n      assert Floki.text(doc |> find(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-empty\"]))) =~\n               \"Hover or select a node\"\n    end\n\n    test \"clicking a node populates the inspector with its name/route/status pill/live stats\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"warning\")\n      render(view)\n\n      html =\n        view\n        |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"/admin/users\"]))\n        |> render_click()\n        |> parsed()\n\n      refute find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-empty\"]))\n      assert Floki.text(find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-name\"]))) =~ \"users\"\n      assert Floki.text(find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-route\"]))) =~ \"/admin/users\"\n\n      pill = find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-status-pill\"]))\n      assert attr(pill, \"data-status\") == \"warn\"\n\n      assert Floki.text(find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-stat-warnings\"]))) =~ \"1\"\n      assert Floki.text(find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-stat-errors\"]))) =~ \"0\"\n    end\n\n    test \"the inspector lists at most the 5 most recent events, newest first\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n\n      for i <- 1..7 do\n        broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\", message: \"event-#{i}\")\n      end\n\n      render(view)\n\n      html =\n        view\n        |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"/admin/users\"]))\n        |> render_click()\n        |> parsed()\n\n      events = find_all(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-event\"]))\n      assert length(events) == 5, \"AC8: expected exactly the last 5 events, got #{length(events)}\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC8: legend — always visible, exactly 6 states\" do\n    test \"the legend renders 6 rows even before any node has been observed\" do\n      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n      doc = parsed(html)\n\n      rows = find_all(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-legend-row\"]))\n      assert length(rows) == 6\n\n      states = Enum.map(rows, &attr(&1, \"data-legend-state\"))\n      assert Enum.sort(states) == Enum.sort([\"ok\", \"warn\", \"error\", \"selected\", \"off\", \"temp\"])\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # AC9: Live stream panel — level filter (leitstand shortcut), resizer\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"AC9: level filter pills default to all-active\" do\n    test \"DEBUG/INFO/WARN/ERROR are all aria-pressed=true on mount\" do\n      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n      doc = parsed(html)\n\n      for level <- [\"DEBUG\", \"INFO\", \"WARN\", \"ERROR\"] do\n        pill = find(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"#{level}\"]))\n        assert pill, \"expected a level pill for #{level}\"\n        assert attr(pill, \"aria-pressed\") == \"true\"\n      end\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC9: leitstand shortcut — click one while all active isolates it; click it again restores all\" do\n    test \"clicking INFO while all 4 are active leaves ONLY INFO active\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n\n      html =\n        view\n        |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"INFO\"]))\n        |> render_click()\n        |> parsed()\n\n      assert attr(find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"INFO\"])), \"aria-pressed\") ==\n               \"true\"\n\n      for level <- [\"DEBUG\", \"WARN\", \"ERROR\"] do\n        pill = find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"#{level}\"]))\n        assert attr(pill, \"aria-pressed\") == \"false\"\n      end\n    end\n\n    test \"clicking the sole active level again restores all 4 to active\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n\n      view\n      |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"INFO\"]))\n      |> render_click()\n\n      html =\n        view\n        |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"INFO\"]))\n        |> render_click()\n        |> parsed()\n\n      for level <- [\"DEBUG\", \"INFO\", \"WARN\", \"ERROR\"] do\n        pill = find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"#{level}\"]))\n        assert attr(pill, \"aria-pressed\") == \"true\"\n      end\n    end\n\n    test \"clicking a level when SEVERAL (not all) are active is a plain toggle, not the shortcut\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n\n      # Isolate INFO (all -> one), then toggle WARN back on: now {INFO, WARN}\n      # are active, i.e. \"several but not all\" — the next click must be a\n      # plain toggle, not re-trigger the shortcut.\n      view |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"INFO\"])) |> render_click()\n\n      view\n      |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"WARN\"]))\n      |> render_click()\n\n      html =\n        view\n        |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"ERROR\"]))\n        |> render_click()\n        |> parsed()\n\n      for level <- [\"INFO\", \"WARN\", \"ERROR\"] do\n        pill = find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"#{level}\"]))\n        assert attr(pill, \"aria-pressed\") == \"true\", \"expected #{level} active\"\n      end\n\n      debug_pill = find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"DEBUG\"]))\n      assert attr(debug_pill, \"aria-pressed\") == \"false\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC9: stream content is filtered by level AND by the active endpoint selection\" do\n    test \"deactivating WARN hides warning-level lines but keeps info-level lines\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\", message: \"distinctive-info-line\")\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"warning\", message: \"distinctive-warn-line\")\n      render(view)\n\n      html =\n        view\n        |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"WARN\"]))\n        |> render_click()\n        |> parsed()\n\n      lines = find_all(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-stream-line\"]))\n      texts = Enum.map(lines, &Floki.text/1)\n\n      assert Enum.any?(texts, &(&1 =~ \"distinctive-info-line\"))\n      refute Enum.any?(texts, &(&1 =~ \"distinctive-warn-line\"))\n    end\n\n    test \"selecting a node's filter narrows the stream to that endpoint's lines\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\", message: \"distinctive-users-line\")\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/rooms\", level: \"info\", message: \"distinctive-rooms-line\")\n      render(view)\n\n      html =\n        view\n        |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"/admin/users\"]))\n        |> render_click()\n        |> parsed()\n\n      texts = html |> find_all(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-stream-line\"])) |> Enum.map(&Floki.text/1)\n\n      assert Enum.any?(texts, &(&1 =~ \"distinctive-users-line\"))\n      refute Enum.any?(texts, &(&1 =~ \"distinctive-rooms-line\"))\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC9: the resizer's static, accessible markup contract\" do\n    test \"role=separator, aria-orientation=horizontal, tabindex=0, has a label\" do\n      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n      resizer = find(parsed(html), ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-resizer\"]))\n\n      assert attr(resizer, \"role\") == \"separator\"\n      assert attr(resizer, \"aria-orientation\") == \"horizontal\"\n      assert attr(resizer, \"tabindex\") == \"0\"\n      assert attr(resizer, \"aria-label\") not in [nil, \"\"]\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # AC10: Accessibility — blocking requirements\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"AC10: every node is a focusable, labeled, toggle-state-exposing element\" do\n    test \"a satellite node's aria-label mentions its route, status, and counts\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"error\")\n\n      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n      node = node_locator(doc, \"/admin/users\")\n\n      assert attr(node, \"role\") == \"button\"\n      assert attr(node, \"tabindex\") == \"0\"\n\n      label = attr(node, \"aria-label\")\n      assert label =~ \"/admin/users\"\n      assert label =~ ~r/error/i\n      assert label =~ ~r/\\d+ (error|warning|request)/\n    end\n\n    test \"nebu's aria-label mentions \\\"all traffic\\\" and its overall status\" do\n      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n      nebu = node_locator(parsed(html), \"nebu\")\n\n      label = attr(nebu, \"aria-label\")\n      assert label =~ \"nebu\"\n      assert label =~ ~r/healthy|ok/i\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC10: a live region announces newly-detected errors\" do\n    test \"the announcer region is present with role=status/aria-live=polite\" do\n      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n      announcer = find(parsed(html), ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-announcer\"]))\n\n      assert announcer\n      assert attr(announcer, \"role\") == \"status\"\n      assert attr(announcer, \"aria-live\") == \"polite\"\n    end\n\n    test \"a fresh error is announced, naming the endpoint and count, with no message content\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n      broadcast_entry(\n        route_template: \"/admin/users\",\n        level: \"error\",\n        message: \"distinctive-should-not-leak-into-announcement\"\n      )\n\n      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n      announcer = find(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-announcer\"]))\n      assert announcer, \"expected the announcer region to be present\"\n\n      text = Floki.text(announcer)\n\n      assert text =~ \"/admin/users\"\n      refute text =~ \"distinctive-should-not-leak-into-announcement\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC10: DOM order places nebu before domains before their satellites (natural Tab order)\" do\n    test \"nebu -> domain -> its satellites, in document order\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\")\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/rooms\", level: \"info\")\n\n      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n      keys = doc |> find_all(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"])) |> Enum.map(&attr(&1, \"data-node-key\"))\n\n      assert Enum.find_index(keys, &(&1 == \"nebu\")) == 0\n      admin_index = Enum.find_index(keys, &(&1 == \"/admin\"))\n      users_index = Enum.find_index(keys, &(&1 == \"/admin/users\"))\n      rooms_index = Enum.find_index(keys, &(&1 == \"/admin/rooms\"))\n\n      assert admin_index < users_index\n      assert admin_index < rooms_index\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # AC11: CSP-compliant client-side behavior (static markup proxy —\n  # real hook wiring is exercised by app.js + logs-trace-map.feature)\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"AC11: no inline <script> tags or inline event-handler attributes anywhere on the page\" do\n    test \"the rendered page contains no INLINE <script> tag (external, src=-bearing tags are fine) and no on*= inline handlers\" do\n      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n\n      # The root layout (Nebu.Admin.Layouts.root/1) legitimately renders several\n      # EXTERNAL `<script src=\"...\">` tags (phoenix.min.js, app.js, ...) — CSP\n      # `script-src 'self'` allows those; AC11 forbids INLINE script content\n      # only (no `src` attribute) — a `<script ...>` tag NOT immediately\n      # followed by a `src=` attribute anywhere before its closing `>`.\n      refute Regex.match?(~r/<script(?![^>]*\\bsrc=)[^>]*>/i, html),\n             \"AC11 (script-src 'self'): no INLINE <script> tag (without a src=...) \" <>\n               \"may appear in server-rendered HTML\"\n\n      refute Regex.match?(~r/\\son[a-z]+\\s*=/i, html),\n             \"AC11: no inline onclick=/onmouseover=/etc. event-handler attribute may appear\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # AC14: UX Gate Advisory A1 — Logs \"Configuration\" child renamed\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"AC14: the Logs submenu's \\\"Configuration\\\" child is disambiguated (Option A: rename)\" do\n    test \"the logs-config nav child's label is no longer the bare word \\\"Configuration\\\"\" do\n      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n      doc = parsed(html)\n\n      logs_config_link = find(doc, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"logs-config\"]))\n      assert logs_config_link, \"expected the Logs submenu to still contain a logs-config child\"\n\n      label = Floki.text(logs_config_link) |> String.trim()\n\n      refute label == \"Configuration\",\n             \"AC14 (default Option A): the Logs child must be renamed away from the bare \" <>\n               \"\\\"Configuration\\\" label (e.g. \\\"Log Settings\\\") to disambiguate it from the \" <>\n               \"top-level Configuration section — see the story's AC14 for Option B (removal) \" <>\n               \"as the alternative, explicit resolution\"\n\n      assert attr(logs_config_link, \"href\") == \"/admin/logs/config\",\n             \"AC14: the route itself is unchanged, only the nav label\"\n    end\n  end\nend\n","numLines":972,"startLine":1,"totalLines":972}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2c41785d-db9d-468d-9c31-c2aec81ae72f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapRetirementTest do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  RED-phase ATDD tests for `enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand`, AC13\n  (retiring the embedded Trace Map from the `/admin/logs` Live Stream page).\n\n  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand.md\n\n  ## Why this MUST FAIL right now\n\n  `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s `render/1` (`server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live.ex`,\n  the \"Endpoints\" section around line 1056) still renders\n  `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapTree.endpoint_tree/1` — the embedded,\n  flat/hierarchical Endpoint-dimension Trace Map this story retires from the\n  Live Stream page in favor of the new dedicated `/admin/logs/trace-map`\n  page. These tests currently FAIL because that markup is exactly what is\n  asserted ABSENT below (the opposite of every existing assertion in\n  `logs_live_test.exs`, which still expects `[data-testid=\"trace-map-tree\"]`\n  to be present — see AC13's own text: \"Update the relevant tests to\n  reflect the removal of endpoint filtering from `/admin/logs`\", which is\n  the dev-story's job for THAT file; this NEW file exists precisely so the\n  ATDD step does not have to hand-edit the large, pre-existing\n  `logs_live_test.exs` suite wholesale).\n\n  ## What AC13 requires, and what stays\n\n    * REMOVED from `/admin/logs`: the \"Endpoints\" dimension section\n      entirely (`TraceMapTree.endpoint_tree/1` and, transitively, every\n      `TraceMapBubble` instance rendered for `dimension: :endpoint`) — this\n      dimension moves to the new radial map page.\n    * KEPT on `/admin/logs`, unchanged: the \"Levels\" and \"Sources\"\n      dimension sections (`TraceMapBubble` instances for `dimension: :level`\n      / `dimension: :source`), the request-stream rows, the detail panel,\n      and the War Room panel — none of those are in scope for this story.\n\n  ## `TraceMapBubble`/`TraceMapTree` component retirement (AC13's own\n  \"Dev Notes\" — component-level decision, not testable here)\n\n  AC13 asks the dev to explicitly DOCUMENT (in the story's own Dev Notes,\n  not in a test) whether `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapBubble` and\n  `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapTree` are kept (reused by the new Leitstand\n  page or its legend) or deleted. This file does not assert either way — it\n  only pins down the OBSERVABLE render contract of `/admin/logs` itself, per\n  the moduledoc-level \"why this MUST FAIL\" note above.\n  \"\"\"\n\n  use ExUnit.Case, async: false\n\n  import Phoenix.LiveViewTest\n\n  @endpoint Nebu.Admin.Endpoint\n  @admin_session %{\"current_user\" => %{\"admin\" => true, \"sub\" => \"admin-trace-map-retirement-tests\"}}\n\n  defp mount_logs_live(session \\\\ @admin_session) do\n    live_isolated(Plug.Test.conn(:get, \"/admin/logs\"), Nebu.Admin.LogsLive, session: session)\n  end\n\n  defp parsed(html), do: Floki.parse_document!(html)\n  defp find(html, selector), do: html |> Floki.find(selector) |> List.first()\n  defp find_all(html, selector), do: Floki.find(html, selector)\n\n  describe \"AC13: the Endpoints dimension (Trace Map tree/bubbles) is gone from /admin/logs\" do\n    test \"no [data-testid=\\\"trace-map-tree\\\"] renders on the Live Stream page\" do\n      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_logs_live()\n      doc = parsed(html)\n\n      refute find(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-tree\"])),\n             \"AC13: the hierarchical Endpoint tree must be removed from /admin/logs \" <>\n               \"(it now lives on the dedicated /admin/logs/trace-map page)\"\n\n      refute find(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-bubble\"][data-bubble-dimension=\"endpoint\"])),\n             \"AC13: no endpoint-dimension bubble may remain on /admin/logs, \" <>\n               \"even as a flat (non-tree) list\"\n    end\n\n    test \"the page no longer advertises an \\\"Endpoints\\\" section heading\" do\n      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_logs_live()\n      doc = parsed(html)\n\n      headings = doc |> find_all(\"h3\") |> Enum.map(&Floki.text/1)\n      refute Enum.any?(headings, &(&1 =~ \"Endpoints\")),\n             \"AC13: the \\\"Endpoints\\\" dimension heading must be removed alongside its content\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC13: the Levels and Sources dimensions are UNCHANGED and still present\" do\n    test \"level bubbles (DEBUG/INFO/WARN/ERROR-equivalent) are still rendered\" do\n      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_logs_live()\n      doc = parsed(html)\n\n      for level <- [\"error\", \"warning\", \"info\", \"debug\"] do\n        assert find(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-bubble\"][data-bubble-dimension=\"level\"][data-bubble-value=\"#{level}\"])),\n               \"AC13: the \\\"#{level}\\\" level bubble must remain on /admin/logs, unchanged\"\n      end\n    end\n\n    test \"source bubbles (http/ecto/other) are still rendered\" do\n      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_logs_live()\n      doc = parsed(html)\n\n      for source <- [\"http\", \"ecto\", \"other\"] do\n        assert find(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-bubble\"][data-bubble-dimension=\"source\"][data-bubble-value=\"#{source}\"])),\n               \"AC13: the \\\"#{source}\\\" source bubble must remain on /admin/logs, unchanged\"\n      end\n    end\n  end\nend\n","numLines":107,"startLine":1,"totalLines":107}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9b7a1365-0e80-4e41-a759-f6502c4e729e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"3f2d6afa-4212-477d-9d20-8d83fece57b4","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"8a10f1fe-70f8-466c-8a7b-d8fc0c9a25e0","agentId":"a4facc758d1de40e1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Fp4rMBjZXn12M9Lk53aQdB","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tFeature: Admin UI — Trace Map Leitstand (dedicated radial health map)\n2\t  As a Nebu platform operator managing production logs\n3\t  I want a dedicated, full-page radial health map at /admin/logs/trace-map\n4\t  So that I can rapidly diagnose \"where is the system red?\" and filter the live stream\n5\t    by clicking nodes, with keyboard and mouse, in an accessible way\n6\t\n7\t  # Story enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand — ATDD, written FIRST (RED phase).\n8\t  # docs/stories/enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand.md\n9\t  # Design: _bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-spec.md\n10\t  # Interactive reference: trace-map-leitstand-dummy.html (same directory).\n11\t  #\n12\t  # RED PHASE: none of `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`, `Nebu.Admin.TraceMapGeometry`,\n13\t  # or the real `/admin/logs/trace-map` route exist yet — the route is still a\n14\t  # stub pointing at the OLD `/admin/logs` page (see router.ex). Every scenario\n15\t  # below is tagged @pending-feature (see playwright.config.ts's DEFAULT_E2E_TAGS,\n16\t  # \"not @pending-feature\") and excluded from the default suite until the\n17\t  # dev-story implements the page — mirrors this project's established\n18\t  # convention (logs-monitoring.feature / logs-trace-map-hierarchy.feature's own\n19\t  # un-tagging history).\n20\t  #\n21\t  # Server-side/component-level coverage of this story's ACs lives in:\n22\t  #   - server/test/nebu/admin/trace_map_geometry_test.exs (AC2 geometry)\n23\t  #   - server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_status_test.exs (AC3 status model)\n24\t  #   - server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_leitstand_test.exs (AC1/AC4-AC11/AC14)\n25\t  #   - server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_retirement_test.exs (AC13)\n26\t  # This file covers the genuinely browser-only half Phoenix.LiveViewTest cannot\n27\t  # exercise: real keyboard focus movement, hover tooltips, pointer drag on the\n28\t  # resizer, and real page-URL/reload round trips.\n29\t  #\n30\t  # KNOWN GAP (flagged for the dev-story, not silently worked around): unlike\n31\t  # the sibling `admin-logs-monitoring-view` page's own established fixture\n32\t  # limitation (see logs-monitoring.feature's header comment — RequestLogger\n33\t  # only ever logs at INFO, regardless of response status), Scenario 2 below\n34\t  # needs a genuine WARN/ERROR-level operational_logs entry, which real black-box\n35\t  # HTTP traffic cannot deterministically produce. \"a warning/error has been\n36\t  # recorded for the {string} endpoint\" is a NEW step (logs-trace-map.steps.ts)\n37\t  # that assumes a test-only log-injection seam does not exist yet and will\n38\t  # need one added by the dev-story (e.g. a test-env-only endpoint, or a\n39\t  # DB-level insert into operational_logs mirroring\n40\t  # logs_live_test.exs's own AC12 raw-SQL-seed precedent) — flagged explicitly\n41\t  # here rather than silently faked.\n42\t\n43\t  Background:\n44\t    Given the Nebu stack is running\n45\t    And bootstrap has been completed\n46\t    And the operator is logged in as admin\n47\t\n48\t  @pending-feature @ac1-route\n49\t  Scenario: Map renders with domains and satellites\n50\t    Given a request to \"/_matrix/client/versions\" has been recorded\n51\t    And a request to \"/_matrix/client/v3/login\" has been recorded\n52\t    When the operator navigates to \"/admin/logs/trace-map\"\n53\t    Then the page shows \"Trace Map\"\n54\t    And I see the \"nebu\" center node on the Trace Map\n55\t    And I see a domain node for \"/_matrix/client\" on the Trace Map\n56\t    And I see a satellite node for \"/_matrix/client/versions\" on the Trace Map\n57\t\n58\t  @pending-feature @ac3-status @ac4-visual-states\n59\t  Scenario: Status colors reflect log entries\n60\t    Given a warning has been recorded for the \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\" endpoint\n61\t that I can rapidly diagnose \"where is the system red?\" and filter the live stream\n    by clicking nodes, with keyboard and mouse, in an accessible way\n\n  # Story enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand — ATDD, written FIRST (RED phase).\n  # docs/stories/enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand.md\n  # Design: router.ex). \"the Nebu stack is running\"                  (common/stack-health.steps.ts)\n *   - \"bootstrap has been completed\"                (admin/bootstrap.steps.ts)\n *   - \"the operator is logged in as admin\"          (admin/bootstrap.steps.ts)\n *   - \"the operator navigates to {string}\"          (admin/bootstrap.steps.ts)\n *   - \"the page shows {string}\"                     (admin/bootstrap.steps.ts)\n *   - \"a request to {string} has been recorded\"     (admin/logs-monitoring.steps.ts)\n *   - \"the page URL contains the query parameter {string}\"\n *                                                    (admin/logs-trace-map-filter-persistence.steps.ts)\n *   - \"the operator starts a War Room with reason {string} and scope {string}\"\n *                                                    (admin/war-room.steps.ts)\n *\n * ## Data-attribute contract this file assumes (mirrors the AUTHORITATIVE\n * contract pinned down server-side by\n * server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_leitstand_test.exs's own\n * moduledoc — kept in sync with that file, not duplicated/reinvented here):\n *   - Node: [data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=...]\n *     [data-node-status=...][data-node-selected=...], role=\"button\",\n *     tabindex=\"0\", aria-pressed, aria-label.\n *   - Filter chip: [data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip\"][data-chip-value=...].\n *   - War-room banner: [data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-banner\"],\n *     dot [data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-dot\"],\n *     open link [data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-open-link\"].\n *   - Tooltip: [data-testid=\"trace-map-tooltip\"].\n *   - Inspector: [data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-name\"/-route/-status-pill/-event].\n *   - Level pill: [data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"DEBUG\"|\"INFO\"|\"WARN\"|\"ERROR\"].\n *   - Resizer: [data-testid=\"trace-map-resizer\"], role=\"separator\".\n *   - Stream line: [data-testid=\"trace-map-stream-line\"][data-route=...].\n *\n * ## KNOWN GAP — see the feature file's own header comment\n *\n * \"a warning/error has been recorded for the {string} endpoint\" assumes a\n * test-only log-injection seam that does not exist yet (RequestLogger only\n * ever logs real HTTP traffic at INFO — see logs-monitoring.steps.ts's own\n * header comment for the identical, already-documented limitation). This\n * step is written against a PROPOSED `POST /admin/_test/operational-logs`\n * backdoor (test-env-only, mirrors no existing route today) — the dev-story\n * must either add that backdoor or replace this step's implementation with\n * whatever mechanism it actually chooses (e.g. a direct DB insert via a\n * Playwright fixture, matching logs_live_test.exs's own raw-SQL-seed\n * precedent for its AC12 describe block). {"parentUuid":"d37e8e5d-8b2b-4932-ab86-adeaca7ee41e","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a4facc758d1de40e1","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5","id":"msg_011Ccnhw9yJ2j3KXNb4RTUuc","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"# Test Review Report — `trace-map-leitstand` (ATDD red phase)\n\n**Verdict: 2 MAJOR, 7 MINOR**\n\nReviewed files (all absolute paths):\n- `/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/test/nebu/admin/trace_map_geometry_test.exs`\n- `/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_status_test.exs`\n- `/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_leitstand_test.exs`\n- `/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_retirement_test.exs`\n- `/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/e2e/features/admin/logs-trace-map.feature`\n- `/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/e2e/step-definitions/admin/logs-trace-map.steps.ts`\n- Plus stale-spec annotations in `e2e/features/admin/logs-monitoring.feature`, `logs-trace-map-filter-persistence.feature`, `logs-trace-map-hierarchy.feature` (comment-only, correctly deferred to dev-story per AC12).\n\nFacts I independently verified: stub route exists at `server/lib/nebu/admin/router.ex:227` (`live \"/logs/trace-map\", LogsLive, :index`); `@pending-feature` is excluded by `DEFAULT_E2E_TAGS` in `e2e/playwright.config.ts:26`; the real LogStore broadcasts a **bare entry map** on `\"operational_logs\"` (`server/lib/nebu/web/log_store/handler.ex:194`) — the tests' `broadcast_entry/1` matches production message shape exactly (note: the story's Dev Notes `{:operational_log_entry, entry}` tuple hint is wrong vs. reality; the tests follow reality — good); old `/admin/logs` renders `<h2>Trace Map</h2>` (`logs_live.ex:1034`); the Logs nav child label is still `\"Configuration\"` (`layouts.ex:54`), so the AC14 test is genuinely red.\n\n## AC Coverage Matrix (all 14 ACs covered — no MAJOR here)\n\n| AC | Coverage | Status |\n|---|---|---|\n| AC1 route/nav | leitstand AC1 block; e2e Scenario 1 | ✓ red (stub sets wrong active-nav) |\n| AC2 radial SVG | geometry_test (21 tests) + leitstand AC2 block + e2e Sc. 1 | ✓ but see MINOR-1 |\n| AC3 status window | status_test (18 tests, deterministic integer clock) + propagation in leitstand + e2e Sc. 2 | ✓ exemplary |\n| AC4 visual states | ok/warn/error/selected + badges; pulse in e2e Sc. 2; off/temp deferred | ✓ see Deviation 3 |\n| AC5 filtering | toggle, multi-select OR, clear-all (nebu + button), chip remove, URL round-trip; e2e Sc. 3 & 10 | ✓ see MINOR-2 |\n| AC6 war-room banner | hidden default + `:integration` active-session test + e2e Sc. 7 | ✓ see MINOR-7 |\n| AC7 tooltip | data-attr contract + no-sensitive-data negative + e2e Sc. 5 | ✓ |\n| AC8 inspector/legend | empty state, populated, 5-event cap, 6-row legend + e2e Sc. 6 | ✓ |\n| AC9 stream panel | pills default, all 3 shortcut branches, level+endpoint stream filtering, resizer markup + e2e Sc. 8/9 (drag, dblclick, ArrowUp/Down, Home/End) | ✓ |\n| AC10 accessibility | aria-labels, announcer + no-leak negative, DOM order, role/tabindex + e2e Sc. 4 keyboard | ✓ see MAJOR-2, MINOR-6 |\n| AC11 CSP | no-inline-script/no-`on*=` invariant | ✓ |\n| AC12 e2e file | 10 scenarios present, `@pending-feature`, stale files annotated | ✓ |\n| AC13 retirement | retirement_test (2 red removals + 2 green regression pins) | ✓ |\n| AC14 A1 label | nav-label test, genuinely red today | ✓ |\n\nKeyboard operability: node activation via Enter/Space (e2e Sc. 4) and resizer arrows/Home/End (e2e Sc. 9) are both covered. URL round-trip: covered server-side (bracket-array mount test, `assert_patch`) and in e2e Sc. 10 with real reload. Hard waits: **none** (no `sleep`/`waitForTimeout`; only auto-retrying `expect` timeouts). Matrix spec error codes: N/A (`matrix: false`, no Matrix API surface). GenServer crash/restart: **N/A by design and correctly so** — `TraceMapStatus` is deliberately a pure functional module (no process), and LiveView crash-recovery semantics are exactly what the URL-round-trip tests pin (state restored from URL params, the root fix of the filter-reset bug class).\n\n## MAJOR findings\n\n**MAJOR-1 — Test backdoor `POST /admin/_test/operational-logs` is an unacceptable design direction (Deviation 4: DISAGREE with the proposed mechanism).**\n`logs-trace-map.steps.ts:102-118` hard-codes two step implementations against a proposed test-only HTTP endpoint in production server code. The comments honestly flag it, but the code is what the dev-story will implement — and a `_test` route inside the pipeline-protected `server/lib/` is a security smell Kassandra will (rightly) reject on a story with `security_review: required`. It is also unnecessary: this repo already has a superior precedent — `logs_live_test.exs`'s AC12 raw-SQL seed. Required fix before dev-story: rewrite both steps as a Playwright fixture doing a **direct DB insert into `operational_logs`** (the integration stack exposes postgres; Scenario 2 reloads the page after each injection, so DB-seeded entries are picked up by the mount-time LogStore history read — no PubSub needed), or a test-env-only mix seed task invoked from the fixture. Deterministic warn-producing real HTTP traffic is not viable (RequestLogger logs only INFO — correctly documented in the feature header), so the DB-seed route is the answer. Optionally tag Scenario 2 `@requires-log-seed` until the dev confirms the seam. No `/admin/_test/*` route may land in `server/lib/nebu/admin/router.ex`.\n\n**MAJOR-2 — e2e Scenario 4's first-Tab assumption cannot pass against a correct implementation.**\n`logs-trace-map.feature:92-94`: \"When the operator presses Tab / Then the 'nebu' node ... has focus\". The admin layout renders a full sidebar (Logs group with three children, per `layouts.ex`) before the main content in document order — the first Tab from a fresh navigation lands in the chrome, not on nebu. A correct, accessible implementation (natural document order, no positive `tabindex`, no focus-stealing) fails this step; the only ways to make it pass are antipatterns. This is inherited from the story's own AC12 Scenario 4 wording, but the test must model reality: replace the first two Tab steps with the already-implemented programmatic-focus step (`the operator tabs to the \"...\" node`, `steps.ts:337`) or a \"presses Tab until a Trace Map node is focused\" loop, then keep the focus-ring, domain-node, Enter and Space assertions unchanged. Flag the wording tension back to the PM so the story's Scenario 4 text is amended.\n\n## MINOR findings\n\n**MINOR-1 — AC2 rendered-geometry cross-check is missing, and the geometry moduledoc falsely claims it exists.** `trace_map_geometry_test.exs:22-27` says the leitstand file's AC2 block contains \"the rendered-output cross-check that keeps this file honest against the real template\" — it does not; the AC2 block asserts only viewBox, presence, and `data-node-kind`. Nothing ties `TraceMapGeometry`'s computed (x, y) to the actual SVG. Add one rendered-position assertion (e.g., a domain `<g>` transform/`cx`/`cy` equals `polar_to_cartesian(-48.0, 195.0)` after observing `/_matrix/client` traffic), or correct the moduledoc.\n\n**MINOR-2 — AC5 domain-convenience stream semantics untested.** Clicking a domain must OR-include its child satellites' stream lines; the domain-click test (`trace_map_leitstand_test.exs:547-562`) asserts only chip presence, and the stream-narrowing test uses a satellite. Add: select `/admin` → stream shows `/admin/users` and `/admin/rooms` lines, hides `/_matrix/*` lines.\n\n**MINOR-3 — Bracket-array URL format (Deviation 2: AGREE, with a required story amendment).** `Plug.Conn.Query.decode/1` is last-wins on repeated bare keys, so AC5/AC12's literal `?endpoint=X&endpoint=Y` cannot round-trip; `?endpoint[]=X&endpoint[]=Y` is the correct, lossless, idiomatic call, applied consistently in the server tests (`assert_patch` at line 414, mount test at 530-538) and e2e Scenario 10 (line 173). But the tests now contradict the story's literal URL examples (story lines 111, 390) — PM must amend AC5/AC12 text or explicitly approve the deviation before the green run, otherwise the test-vs-story mismatch will resurface at review gates.\n\n**MINOR-4 — Incidentally-passing tests (Deviation 6: partially acceptable, two need strengthening).** The 2 retirement regression pins (Levels/Sources kept) are *intentionally* green — acceptable and valuable. The AC11 inline-script invariant passing against the stub is acceptable as a standing guard. Two are genuinely weak: (a) \"the page title mentions Trace Map\" (`html =~ \"Trace Map\"`, leitstand:264-267) passes today against the stub — old `/admin/logs` renders `<h2>Trace Map</h2>` and the sidebar label matches on *every* admin page; assert `page_title` or a page-level `<h1>` instead. (b) \"empty query string selects nothing\" (leitstand:541-544) passes trivially wherever the chip testid is absent; also assert `[data-testid=\"trace-map-svg\"]` presence so it can only pass on the new page.\n\n**MINOR-5 — Resizer accessible-name contract mismatch.** e2e `resizerLocator` requires name `/resize/i` (`steps.ts:89`); the server test only requires a non-empty `aria-label` (leitstand:841). Pin the same contract in both (server assert `aria-label =~ ~r/resize/i`), or the suites can green/red independently on the same markup.\n\n**MINOR-6 — `prefers-reduced-motion` not covered.** AC10 (blocking per spec §8) requires pulse disabled under reduced motion; e2e Scenario 2 only asserts the pulse *without* it. Add a Playwright `emulateMedia({ reducedMotion: 'reduce' })` step asserting `animationName === 'none'`.\n\n**MINOR-7 — AC6 elapsed clock untested.** The banner's live mm:ss timer (phx-hook) is asserted nowhere; e2e Scenario 7 checks reason/dot/link only. Add an e2e assertion that the banner text matches `/\\d{2}:\\d{2}/`.\n\n## INFO / deviation judgments\n\n- **Deviation 1 (separate `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap` module): AGREE — sound.** `live_isolated/3` genuinely refuses LiveViews defining `handle_params/3`, AC5 hard-requires `handle_params/3`, and AC1's \"(or alias)\" explicitly permits the split. One correction: the \"~200 live_isolated tests\" claim is inflated — actual count is ~40 (`logs_live_test.exs` 33 + `logs_controller_test.exs` + component tests). The argument holds regardless; fix the number in the moduledoc.\n- **Deviation 3 (off/temp excluded, legend still 6 rows): AGREE — consistent.** The legend is a *static* always-visible card whose 6 rows are enumerated verbatim in AC8; off/temp *node behavior* is deferred by the story's own Known Gaps section, and no endpoint is off-tier-classified by this story, so there is no reachable off/temp scenario. Recommend PM annotates AC4's table with the deferral to close the paper trail.\n- **Deviation 5 (`apply/3` indirection): AGREE — acceptable red-phase pattern** given `--warnings-as-errors` xref checks on plain modules; direct calls would turn \"fail at runtime\" into \"fail to compile\" for the whole suite. Recommendation: dev-story converts the wrappers to direct calls once the modules exist.\n- **Determinism: exemplary.** Integer-millisecond clock in `trace_map_status_test.exs` (window boundary tested at exactly 59_999/60_000ms), no sleeps, no wall-clock reads, auto-retrying Playwright expects only. Module-level `lastStreamHeight` in `steps.ts:502` is worker-shared mutable state — safe today (first use always re-seeds) but fragile if Scenario 9 is ever split; consider a `test.info()`-scoped fixture.\n- **Dead step definition:** bare `the operator presses Enter` (`steps.ts:286`) is unused by the feature file — remove to reduce ambiguity surface. No actual bddgen collisions found with existing step files (sidebar-nesting and element steps use distinct patterns).\n- **Negative auth test:** the new route sits in the existing protected live_session; scope-level auth is covered elsewhere, but a one-line \"unauthenticated GET /admin/logs/trace-map redirects\" test would be cheap insurance.\n\n## Summary for the pipeline\n\nThe server-side ATDD suite is unusually strong: deterministic clock design, negative sensitive-data assertions (tooltip, announcer), correct PubSub message-shape mirroring, honest red-phase documentation, and full 14/14 AC coverage. The two MAJORs are both confined to the e2e layer and are cheap to fix before the dev-story starts: (1) replace the proposed HTTP backdoor with a DB-seed fixture per the in-repo raw-SQL precedent, (2) replace Scenario 4's first-Tab assumption with programmatic focus.
